### [1] [Stability analysis of the Lane--Emden equation of index $n=5$ describing polytropic gas spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35932)
*S. C. Mancas,Ronald Adams*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了指数 n=5 的 Lane–Emden 方程（区分有限半径与无限半径自引力平衡态的临界多方情形），完整刻画了其动力学结构、解的封闭形式、奇解的稳定性以及 Schuster 廓线的谱性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界多方指数 n=5 是自引力平衡态由有限半径过渡到无限半径的分界点，此前该情形的解结构、奇解稳定性及线性化算子的谱性质缺乏完整刻画，作者旨在填补这一空白并提供解析与数值相印证的结果。

Method: 首先利用标度族生成 Schuster 廓线的中性模式；通过 Schuster 变换和对数变量将原方程化为自治 Hamilton 系统，并用第一积分求得穿越原点的轨道；用同调不变量分析奇异解的稳定性；对带平移平衡点的距离变化 Emden–Fowler 系统证明非线性不稳定性；最后将 Schuster 廓线线性化研究 Schrödinger 算子 L 及其 Friedrichs 扩张的谱。

Result: 除 Schuster（分界线）解和 Srivastava 解外，所有穿越 ν=0 的轨道均可由 Weierstrass ℘-函数给出封闭形式，并与数值积分符合到约 10^{-12}；在标度与符号意义下 Schuster 解是唯一在原心正则的解，且分隔正解与具有无穷多零点的解；奇异解在 n=5 处是稳定性质发生变化的中心；n=5 时唯一平衡点线性与非线性不稳定；线性化算子 L 的 Friedrichs 扩张具有本质谱 [0,∞)、由不可归一化标度模式承载的 0 处阈值共振，以及唯一负特征值 λ0≈-1.21，故 Schuster 廓线是能量泛函的鞍点。

Conclusion: n=5 的 Lane–Emden 方程可通过自治 Hamilton 系统完全理解，解的封闭形式可由 Weierstrass 函数表达且与数值高度吻合；Schuster 解在正则解中唯一并起分界作用，奇异解在该指数处恰为稳定性变化的中心；线性化算子的谱显示 Schuster 廓线为鞍点，从而揭示了这一临界情形的精细动力学与稳定性图景。

Abstract: We study the Lane--Emden equation of index $n=5$, the critical polytropic case separating finite-radius from infinite-radius self-gravitating equilibria. The scaling family $θ_λ(ξ)=λ^{1/2}θ(λξ)$ generates a neutral mode $φ_0=\tfrac12θ+ξθ'$ about the Schuster profile. Schuster's transformation and a logarithmic variable reduce the equation to the autonomous Hamiltonian system $\ddotν=\tfrac14(ν-ν^5)$ with a first integral. Besides the Schuster (separatrix) and Srivastava solutions, every orbit crossing $ν=0$ is given in closed form by a Weierstrass $\wp$-function, and the closed forms agree with direct numerical integration to about $10^{-12}$. Up to scaling and sign, the Schuster solution is the only solution regular at the center, and it separates positive solutions from solutions with infinitely many zeros. In homology-invariant variables the singular solution $θ=(2ξ)^{-1/2}$ is a center exactly at $n=5$, where its stability changes. For a distance-varying Emden--Fowler system with a shifted equilibrium we prove that at $n=5$ the unique equilibrium is linearly and nonlinearly unstable. Finally, the linearization about the Schuster profile is the Schrödinger operator $L=-Δ_r-5θ_S^4$ on $L^2((0,\infty),ξ^2dξ)$. Its Friedrichs extension has essential spectrum $[0,\infty)$, a threshold resonance at $0$ carried by the non-normalizable scaling mode, and exactly one negative eigenvalue, $λ_0\approx-1.21$, so the Schuster profile is a saddle point of the associated energy functional.

</details>


### [2] [Universal Critical Jump for Inverse-Square Ising Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36131)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了平移不变铁磁 Ising 链（满足 n²J_n→B>0）的临界关系 2β_c B m_c²=1，并推广到连续时间正 Stieltjes 类的 Ohmic 自旋-玻色子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究长程一维铁磁 Ising 链在临界耦合处的相变特征，尤其是不依赖有限核心或尾部收敛速率的普适临界关系，以及非零磁化跳变情况下磁化率的特殊行为。

Method: 对宏观界面能量进行上下界估计，证明若临界乘积超过 1 则排除宏观界面存在的可能性；进而讨论连通磁化率的发散性、临界点处剩余磁化随外场趋零时的衰减速率；最后将该跳变关系拓展到连续时间的 Ohmic 自旋-玻色子模型。

Result: 证明了 2β_c B m_c²=1 无条件成立；尽管磁化存在非零跳变，连通磁化率在临界点及其邻近有序区间内均为无穷大；临界耦合处剩余磁化随外场趋零衰减得比场的任意正幂次都慢；并给出连续时间情形的跳变关系。

Conclusion: 该普适临界关系与模型细节无关，揭示了长程一维铁磁系统中临界参数之间的精确约束，并将结论成功推广到一类量子（自旋-玻色子）模型。

Abstract: For every translation-invariant ferromagnetic Ising chain with $n^2J_n\to B>0$, we prove $2β_cBm_c^2=1$, without restrictions on the finite core or the tail convergence rate. The proof bounds the energy of macroscopic interfaces and rules them out if the critical product exceeds one. Despite the nonzero jump, connected susceptibility is infinite both at criticality and in a neighboring ordered interval. At the critical coupling, the excess magnetization decays more slowly than any positive power of the field as the field tends to zero. We also prove the jump relation in continuous time, for a positive-Stieltjes class of Ohmic spin-boson models.

</details>


### [3] [One-dimensional second-gradient elasticity: pantograph-inspired energies and solitary waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36198)
*R. S. Hallyburton,I. Giorgio,C. Rodriguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文自洽地建立了二阶梯度连续体中一维纵向运动的有限应变理论，借助虚功率原理推导控制方程与力学能量恒等式，并通过行波首次积分构造了满足正伸长、指数衰减与有限能量的孤立波解，给出存在性与唯一性判据；在合适弹簧能量下，首次积分约化为多项式，显式给出Green-Lagrange与Hencky应变的拉压孤立波剖面，并证明所构造Hencky能量具有良好的物理与数学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受pantographic微结构启发，研究二阶梯度连续体中由归一化应变测度和形变依赖的应变梯度模量刻画的有限应变能量，为理解控制高阶梯度弹性连续体的丰富非线性色散方程提供一个来源明确、数学基础相对简单的切入途径。

Method: 从有限应变运动学出发，应用虚功率原理确定控制场方程与接触作用，推导平衡律并建立超弹材料的机械能量恒等式；针对特定有限应变能量，通过行波首次积分得到孤立波存在的转折点判据，并在适当弹簧能量下通过应变变量变换将首次积分化为多项式方程，显式求解Green-Lagrange与Hencky应变下的拉压孤波剖面；进而证明Hencky能量非负、自然状态为唯一全局极小、线性化模量为正、惩罚伸长梯度且在材料塌缩与无限延伸下发散，且储能关于对数应变的H^1范数具有强制性。

Result: 得到正伸长、指数衰减、有限机械能量的孤立波存在性判据及固定波速下给定应变区间内的唯一性；显式构造了Green-Lagrange与Hencky应变的拉伸与压缩孤立波剖面；证明Hencky能量具有非负性、自然状态唯一全局极小、正线性化模量、惩罚伸长梯度、在塌缩与无限延伸下发散等性质，并对数应变H^1范数强制；结合机械能量守恒，得到光滑解在整个生命周期上伸长的均匀上界与正下界。

Conclusion: 该理论以可及的力学来源、相对简单的数学基础与多样的孤立波解，为研究控制高阶梯度弹性连续体的丰富非线性色散方程提供了一个易入手的起点。

Abstract: We present a self-contained formulation of longitudinal motion in one-dimensional second-gradient continua. Starting from finite-strain kinematics, we then apply the Principle of Virtual Power to identify the governing field equation and contact actions, derive the balance laws, and establish the mechanical-energy identity for hyperelastic materials. Motivated by pantographic microstructures, we study a class of finite-strain energies formulated in terms of normalized strain measures and deformation-dependent strain-gradient moduli. For the associated equation of motion, a traveling-wave first integral yields a turning-point criterion for the existence of solitary waves with positive stretch, exponential decay, and finite mechanical energy, as well as uniqueness at each fixed speed within a prescribed strain interval. For suitable spring energies, a change of strain variable reduces this integral to a polynomial equation, yielding explicit tensile and compressive profiles for Green--Lagrange and Hencky strains under appropriate parameter and kinematic conditions. Under the positivity condition on the quartic potential, the constructed Hencky energy has several physically natural properties: it is nonnegative, admits the natural state as its unique zero and global minimizer, has a positive linearized modulus, penalizes stretch gradients, and diverges under both material collapse and unlimited extension. Furthermore, the stored energy is coercive with respect to the $H^1$ norm of logarithmic strain. Together with mechanical-energy conservation, this yields uniform upper and positive lower bounds on stretch throughout the lifespan of smooth solutions. The theory's accessible mechanical origin, relatively simple mathematical foundation, and diverse solitary waves offer a tractable entry point to studying the rich nonlinear dispersive equations governing higher-gradient elastic continua.

</details>


### [4] [Propagation Estimates for the Many-body Boson Star Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36388)
*Sébastien Breteaux,Charlotte Dietze,Jérémy Faupin,Viviana Grasselli,Jinyeop Lee*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究平均场机制下的多体玻色星方程，在有效单体方程解满足衰减假设的条件下，证明了一致（时间无关）的迹范数估计，并由此建立多体动力学的最大与最小速度界，进而通过取平均场极限恢复单体玻色星方程的相应速度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多体玻色星方程在平均场极限下动力学性质的长时间行为，特别是想将多体系统中诸如 Lieb--Robinson 型界限等结构信息传递到有效的单体方程上，从而为单体玻色星方程的时间演化提供速度传播控制。

Method: 在有效单体方程的解满足衰减假设的前提下，证明多体态的单粒子约化密度矩阵与单体解投影之间的一致（对时间一致）迹范数估计；利用该估计建立多体动力学的最大速度界（视为 Lieb--Robinson 型界）与最小速度界；再通过取平均场极限（粒子数趋于无穷）将多体结果传递回单体玻色星方程。

Result: 证明了当粒子数趋于无穷时的一致（时间无关）平均场收敛，即单粒子约化密度矩阵一致收敛到单体解的投影；同时为多体动力学建立了最大速度界（Lieb--Robinson 型）和最小速度界；作为应用，通过取平均场极限恢复了单体玻色星方程的最大和最小速度界。

Conclusion: 在平均场机制下，多体玻色星动力学与有效单体方程之间存在一致的时间演化对应关系，且多体系统的速度传播信息可以严格传递到单体极限，从而为玻色星方程的解提供了新的长时间动力学控制。

Abstract: We study the many-body boson star equation in the mean-field regime. Under a decay assumption on the solution to the effective one-body equation, we prove a uniform-in-time trace-norm estimate comparing the one-particle reduced density matrix of the many-body state with the projection onto the one-body solution. This establishes uniform-in-time mean-field convergence as the particle number goes to infinity. Furthermore, for the many-body dynamics, we establish both a maximal velocity bound, which can be seen as a Lieb--Robinson-type bound, and a minimal velocity bound. As an application, we recover maximal and minimal velocity bounds for the one-body boson star equation by passing to the mean-field limit.

</details>


### [5] [Existence of a third self-similar solution of the Yang--Mills equation in high odd dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36784)
*Piotr Bizoń,Robert Kluszczyński*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在任意奇数维 d≥11 中证明第三类光滑自相似 Yang–Mills profile 的存在性，通过端点符号计算与代数论证完成，但一般情形下的唯一性仍待解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bizoń、Glogić 和 Wasserman (BGW) 将奇数维 d≥11 中光滑自相似 Yang–Mills profile 的分类归结为对某显式多项式在指定区间内根的计数问题，并猜想除两个显式 profile 对应的根外恰有一个额外根。本文旨在证明该额外根的存在性。

Method: 证明结合了两部分：一是对共振多项式进行端点符号计算，二是用代数论证表明第三根与两个显式根不同。

Result: 在每一个奇数维 d≥11 中都证明存在一个额外根，从而证明了第三类光滑自相似 profile 的存在。

Conclusion: 第三类光滑自相似 Yang–Mills profile 在所有奇数维 d≥11 中均存在；但该额外根在一般情形下的唯一性仍未解决。

Abstract: In a recent paper~\cite{BGW}, Bizoń, Glogić, and Wasserman (BGW) reduced the classification of smooth self-similar Yang--Mills profiles in odd spatial dimensions $d\ge11$ to an algebraic problem of counting roots of an explicit polynomial in a prescribed interval. Two roots correspond to explicit profiles. BGW conjectured that there is exactly one additional root and verified this in a range of odd dimensions. Here, we prove the existence of an additional root, and hence of a third smooth self-similar profile, in every odd dimension $d\geq11$. The proof combines an endpoint sign calculation for the resonance polynomial with an algebraic argument showing that the third root is distinct from the two explicit ones. Uniqueness remains open in general.

</details>

*Stefano Rossi,Sergio Simonella,Aleksis Vuoksenmaa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在受动理论启发的标度极限族中研究经典相互作用粒子系统的非平衡混沌态，利用簇展开方法对任意阶截断相关函数（累积量）导出逐点界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学理论中，粒子系统的非平衡混沌态及其随粒子数增长的相关函数衰减行为是核心研究对象；作者希望统一处理稀释与非稀释两种标度体系，并涵盖更一般的相互作用势（包括有界宏观区域中不可积尾部的 Riesz 势），从而获得任意阶累积量的可控逐点估计。

Method: 采用同时重新标度粒子活性（activity）与对相互作用的强度，构造一族标度极限；借助簇展开（cluster expansion）技术，对稳定的有限程相互作用与排斥 Riesz 势（含不可积尾部情形）推导任意阶截断相关函数（累积量）的逐点界。

Result: 在覆盖稳定有限程相互作用及排斥 Riesz 势（包括有界宏观域内非可积尾部）的条件下，成功得到任意阶截断相关函数（累积量）的逐点界，统一适用于稀释和非稀释两种标度机制。

Conclusion: 簇展开方法为分析非平衡混沌态的任意阶累积量提供了有效工具，所建立的逐点界在稀释与非稀释标度下均成立，并适用于一类含不可积尾部的 Riesz 势，拓宽了动力学理论中相关函数估计的适用范围。

Abstract: We consider nonequilibrium chaotic states of classical interacting particles in a family of scaling limits motivated by kinetic theory. The particle activity and the strength of the pair interaction are rescaled simultaneously, allowing for both dilute and non-dilute regimes. Using cluster expansion, we derive pointwise bounds for truncated correlation functions (cumulants) of arbitrary order. We cover stable finite-range interactions and repulsive Riesz potentials, including non-integrable tails in bounded macroscopic domains.

</details>


### [7] [Threshold Criteria for Bosonic and Fermionic Trimers on a Square Lattice: A Birman--Schwinger Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37142)
*Janikul Abdullaev,Abdumalik Eshniyozov,Mikhail Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了正方晶格上总准动量为零的两种三粒子模型：具有吸引接触相互作用的三全同玻色子，以及具有吸引种间接触的二费米子加一可区分粒子系统。通过 Birman–Schwinger 原理、对称性约化与强耦合渐近分析，区分了共通方法与模型相关的谱结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清在低维晶格三体问题中，哪些分析方法是通用的、哪些谱性质因统计与质量比而异，从而与已有的数值 Faddeev 计算及三维阈值态分类结果对照。

Method: 采用 Birman–Schwinger 原理结合对称性约化和强耦合渐近展开，对两种模型在总准动量为零下的束缚态与阈值行为进行严格分析，并与数值有限格点 Faddeev 计算及三维阈值态分类进行比较。

Result: 玻色子情形：基态能量为负三倍耦合加六再减去二倍耦合分之三加高阶修正，强耦合下阈值以下恰有两个束缚态，第二束缚态位于阈值下方有限距离 0.03542 处，为真本征值而非阈值共振。费米子情形：临界质量比为 π/(π-2) ≈ 2.75194，超过该值时强耦合下出现二重简并能级；在阈值附近，结合能遵循线性除以对数的规律。

Conclusion: 共通方法（Birman–Schwinger 原理与强耦合渐近）可统一处理两类模型，但谱结论强烈依赖于粒子统计与质量比：玻色子表现为两个真束缚态及特定的基态能量渐近，费米子则在临界质量比之上出现简并能级与对数修正的结合能律。

Abstract: We compare two three-particle lattice models on the square lattice at total quasimomentum zero: three identical bosons with attractive contact interactions, and a two-fermion plus one distinguishable particle system with attractive interspecies contact. Using the Birman--Schwinger principle combined with symmetry reduction and strong-coupling asymptotics, we distinguish shared methodology from model-dependent spectral conclusions. For bosons, the ground-state energy behaves as minus three times the coupling plus six minus three over twice the coupling plus higher-order corrections, and exactly two bound states exist below the threshold for large coupling; the second lies at a finite distance 0.03542 below the threshold, a genuine eigenvalue rather than a threshold resonance. For fermions, the critical mass ratio is pi divided by pi minus two, approximately 2.75194; above it, a doubly degenerate level appears at strong coupling. Near onset, the binding energy follows a linear-over-logarithmic law. The results are compared with numerical finite-grid Faddeev computations and with recent three-dimensional classifications of threshold states.

</details>


### [8] [Removing Trace Preservation from a Universal Spectral Bound for 2-Positive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37187)
*Petri Tanninen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 作者证明了 Vom Ende 等人的猜想：每个条件 2-正映射 L 都满足迹不等式 Tr L ≤ d·m(L) + (d²−d)·s(L)，其中 m(L) 为最小特征值实部、s(L) 为最大特征值实部。证明借助量子 Doob 变换将映射按 Hilbert-Schmidt 伴随的正定特征矩阵归一化为 2-正迹保持映射，无需不可约性或忠实不变态等假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的通用弛豫速率界 Tr L ≤ d·m(L) 仅对迹保持半群的生成元成立，Vom Ende 等人将其推广到非迹保持情形并提出猜想：对每个条件 2-正映射 L 应有 Tr L ≤ d·m(L) + (d²−d)·s(L)。本文的目标是证明这一猜想，并验证常数 d 的最优性。

Method: 构造归一化技巧：取映射 Hilbert-Schmidt 伴随的一个正定特征矩阵，用它实施类似于量子 Doob 变换的归一化，把 2-正映射化为 2-正迹保持映射，从而套用已有的迹保持结论。为保证特征矩阵存在，先对映射加上任意小的非归一化去极化映射的倍数，得到不等式后再由谱的连续性取极限。该方法不要求不可约性、可对角化或忠实不变态。

Result: 证明了猜想：任意条件 2-正映射 L 满足 Tr L ≤ d·m(L) + (d²−d)·s(L)；并证明常数 d 在非迹保持完全正半群类内是最优的。进一步将该结论重新表述为以主导谱增长率衡量的一个关于衰减率的普适约束。

No irreducibility, diagonalizability or faithful-invariant-state assumption is required. We also show that the constant $d$ is optimal within the class of non-trace-preserving completely positive semigroups, and we recast the result as a universal constraint on decay rates measured relative to the dominant spectral growth rate.

Conclusion: 决定是否适用平均场指数的关键因素是沿层级的耦合衰减而非空间维数；层级网络在衰减指数小于维数时自然成为小世界网络，无需像欧氏理论那样假设长程耦合。

Abstract: The Buice-Cowan field theory [PRE 75, 051919 (2007)] describes fluctuations and correlations of neural activity through a path integral whose mean-field limit is the Wilson-Cowan equation and whose critical behavior belongs to the directed percolation class. In its continuum limit the neurons are the points of a Euclidean space and activity spreads by ordinary diffusion, which encodes neither the hierarchical organization of cortical networks nor their small-world property. We formulate a version of the theory in which the neurons are organized in an infinite, self-similar, tree-like structure, described by the p-adic numbers. The path integral carries over unchanged, and finite networks are exact restrictions of the continuum theory. The main change is in the linear response: in the Euclidean theory every short-range kernel reduces to the Laplacian, while in the p-adic theory each connectivity kernel gives its own nonlocal p-adic diffusion operator. For couplings that decay by a fixed factor per level of the hierarchy this is the Taibleson-Vladimirov operator, and the critical theory is an ultrametric Reggeon field theory whose upper critical dimension is twice the decay exponent of the coupling. Hence the decay of the coupling up the hierarchy, and not the dimension of space, decides whether mean-field exponents apply. We obtain a Ginzburg-type criterion, loop-corrected hierarchical Wilson-Cowan equations, log-periodic corrections to scaling and power-law correlations. Exact simulations on a tree support the predictions: the avalanche-size exponent takes the mean-field value 3/2 when the coupling decays slowly and decreases toward the directed-percolation value when it decays fast. The hierarchical network is also small-world when the decay exponent is smaller than the dimension, a property that the Euclidean theory obtains only by assuming long-range coupling.

</details>

Result: 在p-adic层级结构下，上临界维数由N_c=2σ给出（而非传统短程的N_c=4）。树上精确模拟显示：当σ≤N/2时，尺寸指数τ_S=3/2；当σ>N/2时，τ_S从3/2开始连续减小。

Abstract: Neural avalanches with size exponent $3/2$ are usually explained by mean-field critical branching, which for short-range coupling requires dimension $N\ge4$. We show that hierarchical connectivity removes this constraint. In a $p$-adic version of the Buice--Cowan field theory, a coupling that decays by $p^{-(N+σ)}$ per hierarchical level selects the Taibleson--Vladimirov operator, and the critical theory is ultrametric directed percolation with upper critical dimension $N_c=2σ$. Exact simulations on a tree give $τ_S=3/2$ for $σ\le N/2$, decreasing continuously for $σ>N/2$.

</details>

Abstract: What is the intrinsic reason of emergence of macroscopic order parameter out of microscopic systems? In this article, we apply to physics the collective predictive coding (CPC), the theory of language/symbol emergence. As our language emerges out of our human communications, physical macroscopic order parameter emerges out of communications among atoms --- each atom is regarded as an agent who predicts each other's behavior through inference. We present three examples of classical spin systems: two-dimensional ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Ising models, a two-dimensional spin model with competing stripe orders, and the fully connected Sherrington--Kirkpatrick spin-glass model. In all of these examples, proper macroscopic order parameters including the replica overlap emerge automatically through communication informatics of CPC, only from observed spin configurations. We call the learned macroscopic coordinate the collective predictive order. Furthermore, for the ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Ising models, the equilibrium distribution of the collective predictive order yields an emergent potential with the symmetry and thermal evolution characteristic of a Landau potential. Notably, the emergence of the collective predictive order does not refer to any structure of microscopic Hamiltonians/Lagrangians. Thus our ``Atom-as-an-Agent" (AaaA) principle offers a completely data-driven method for the emergence of macroscopic order, that is, machine learning physics.

</details>

Result: 成功克服了 GLM 推广到 VGLM 过程中因所有序参量维度升高而产生的数学困难，证明了该新问题在副本方法下依然可解，建立了 VGLM 过拟合的一般理论，并通过若干相关 VGLM 回归模型验证了理论的正确性与价值。

Conclusion: 该工作将过拟合的统计物理理论从 GLM 扩展到 VGLM，为后基因组医学推断和数据分析等对过拟合敏感的应用开辟了新的可能性，有望缓解过拟合这一长期阻碍进展的关键障碍。

Abstract: We show how the successful recent statistical physics based theory of overfitting in Generalized Linear Models (GLMs) can be generalized to a much larger family of regression models: the so-called Vector Generalized Linear Models (VGLMs), in which not a single linear combination of covariates drives the outcome, but where one has an arbitrary finite number of such linear combinations. The latter more general and complex models can describe more sophisticated statistical patterns in data, but are even more prone to overfitting than GLMs due to their significantly larger number of parameters; this often limits their use. We show that the generalization of the overfitting theory from GLMs to VGLMs causes many mathematical challenges that were absent from the earlier work, mostly related to the increased dimensional complexity of all order parameters in the theory, but that the new problem is once more solvable using the replica method. This opens up the field to many novel and welcome applications, especially in post-genome medical inference and data analysis, where overfitting is and remains a major barrier to progress. After developing the general VGLM overfitting theory, within the replica symmetric (RS) ansatz, we illustrate its merits and its correctness via application to several relevant VGLM regression models.

</details>

*Lukas Péron,Louise Budzynski*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究基于过去耦合（CFTP）和边界链的Ising自旋玻璃完美采样算法，在多种图族和耦合分布上分析其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完美采样（从精确平稳分布中无偏采样）对自旋玻璃这类具有复杂能量景观和缓慢混合特性的模型极具挑战。现有基于边界链的CFTP算法在不同图结构上的收敛行为尚不清楚，需要刻画其可扩展性和失效机制，进而为设计更好的完美采样策略提供依据。

Result: 理论结果：单点边界链在Dobrushin条件下以O(n log n)更新合并；分块边界链在G(n,d/n)上、d足够大的树唯一性区间内以O(n log n)次分块更新合并。数值结果：稀疏随机图上高效采样贯穿整个树唯一性区间，超过该阈值后出现减速；周期格子上单点边界链受限于边界包络的松弛性；Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型上，在所有测试的逆温度下缓慢合并都伴随损伤扩散（damage spreading）。

Conclusion: 边界链完美采样在树唯一性区间内可高效工作，但其性能受制于边界包络的松弛性和损伤扩散现象。这些观察提示需要替代的边界表示与耦合方式，作为改进自旋玻璃完美采样的策略。

Abstract: We study perfect sampling algorithms for Ising spin glasses based on coupling from the past and bounding chains, on several graph families and coupling distributions. For the single-site bounding chain, we establish coalescence in $O(n\log n)$ updates under Dobrushin conditions. On sparse random graphs, our numerical results suggest that efficient sampling extends throughout the tree-uniqueness regime, with a slowdown beyond this threshold. On Erdős-Rényi random graphs $\mathcal{G}(n,d/n)$ with standard Gaussian couplings, we introduce a block bounding chain and prove its coalescence in $O(n\log n)$ block updates in the tree-uniqueness regime for sufficiently large $d$, each block update requiring $O(n)$ operations, adapting a proof in~\cite{Efthymiou_Zampetakis_2024} for the rapid mixing of block Glauber dynamics. On periodic lattices, our experiments instead suggest that the single-site bounding chain is limited by the looseness of its bounding envelope, whereas on the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model, slow coalescence is accompanied by damage spreading at every inverse temperature tested. These observations motivate alternative bounding representations and couplings as strategies to improve perfect sampling on spin glasses.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出"混沌图像"（chaos picture）作为旋玻璃的新理论框架，通过钉扎与动态过滤随机液滴形成层级能量景观，在降温过程中实现标度依赖液滴的逐步冻结，并与相空间中层级自由能景观的分裂过程相对应，从而调和了40年来的液滴理论（droplet）与副本对称破缺（RSB）之争，并对老化、回复与记忆效应及亚稳态（metastate）框架给出统一解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋玻璃领域长期存在液滴理论与副本对称破缺两大理论框架之争，持续40余年未获统一；本文旨在提供一个一致、直观的新框架（混沌图像）来调和这一争议，并解释旋玻璃中的动力学现象。

Method: 作者首先论证通过钉扎（pinning）与随机液滴的动态过滤形成层级能量景观；随着温度降低，混沌图像描述实空间中依赖标度的液滴的渐进式冻结，这一过程与相空间中层级自由能景观的分裂（分叉）相镜像；该分叉伴随温度混沌（temperature chaos），在不违反液滴标度律的前提下实现动力学纯态共存。关键点在于界面几何并非该机制的核心，从而自然纳入界面从平面、分形到空间填充的交叉行为，并与平均场极限一致。

Result: 混沌图像成功调和了液滴理论与副本对称破缺之争：层级冻结过程对应自由能景观分叉，伴随温度混沌实现纯态共存且不破坏液滴标度；界面交叉随维度增加从平面、分形过渡到空间填充，与平均场极限一致；该框架还能解释老化、回复、记忆效应等旋玻璃动力学现象，并为复杂的亚稳态框架提供了简化的重新诠释。

Conclusion: 混沌图像是一个新颖、一致且直观的理论框架，能够统一旋玻璃中液滴与副本对称破缺两大理论图景；其机制不依赖界面几何的临界行为，可自然涵盖不同维度下的界面交叉与平均场极限，并对旋玻璃动力学（老化、回复、记忆）及亚稳态框架提供深刻而简洁的解释。

Abstract: The chaos picture is a novel, coherent, and intuitive theoretical framework for spin glasses, reconciling the 40-year-old droplet vs. replica symmetry breaking debate. First, we argue the formation of a hierarchical energy landscape by pinning and dynamic filtering of random droplets. As the temperature decreases, the chaos picture describes a progressive freezing of scale-dependent droplets in real space, which mirrors a hierarchical free energy landscape fracturing process in phase space. This bifurcation process is accompanied by temperature chaos, leading to dynamic pure state coexistence without violating the droplet scaling. Crucially, the interface geometry is not critical to these mechanisms, naturally incorporating the interface crossover from planar, fractal, to space-filling as the dimensionality increases, in line with the mean-field limit. Finally, the chaos picture provides profound insights into spin-glass dynamics such as aging, rejuvenation, and memory effect, while offering a streamlined reinterpretation of the complex metastate framework.

</details>

### [21] [Bayesian Inference in Partially Observed Critical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35973)
*Mihika Singhania*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究在大规模随机系统中，需要观测多少样本才能在受控不确定性下推断全局平均值。作者指出除采样误差外，还存在一个源于“仅从平衡态抽取单个观测构型”的固有下限误差（称为“底限”R_n），两者共同决定了可认证的容差下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计推断通常只关注采样误差随样本量 m 的减小（如 1/√m 的缩放律），却忽略了另一类更根本的误差来源：当观测对象本身就是从某个概率分布（如洗牌后的牌组、统计物理的平衡态构型、随机场）中抽取的单个实现时，即使对全部对象进行普查（census），也无法消除由该随机性带来的波动。作者希望系统刻画这种“底限”误差，并理解其对贝叶斯推断、统计物理临界现象及传感器网络估计的影响。

Method: 作者以随机对换洗牌（random transposition shuffle）为原型模型，构造位移均值的共轭贝叶斯估计，利用全方差定律（law of total variance）将总方差分解为采样误差项（1/(2m)）与底限项 R_n；进一步推导了该洗牌模型下 R_n 的精确表达式，并验证后验分布的校准性。随后将同一框架推广到二维 Ising 模型的临界点（讨论能量与磁化强度两个观测量）、平均场极限，以及传感器网络读取的相关场，并与单链蒙特卡洛诊断方法做对照，说明从单次实现界定底限所需的相关性衰减假设。

Result: 随机对换洗牌中，由 m 张均匀抽样卡牌得到的平均位移共轭贝叶斯估计，其相对不确定性为 1/√(2m)，与牌组规模 n 无关；底限为 R_n=(2n²+7)/(5(n−1)²(n+1))，且后验保持校准。二维 Ising 模型临界点处，能量的底限趋于零，而磁化强度的底限保持在约 0.072，其平均场极限为 √2−1。对传感器网络读取的相关场，常规 95% 区间对总体均值的实际覆盖率仅为 7%。从单次实现界定底限（如单链 MCMC 诊断）需要对相关性衰减作出假设。

Conclusion: 全局平均值的推断受到两类误差的共同制约：随样本量衰减的采样误差，以及由“单个平衡构型”这一根本随机性决定的底限误差。底限误差决定了任何方法（包括普查）都无法突破的容差下限，且在临界系统（如 Ising 磁化强度）中持续存在。这一发现表明，标准置信区间在相关场情形下可能严重失准，而可靠界定底限必须依赖关于相关性衰减的外在假设。

Abstract: We ask how much of a large random system must be observed before a global average can be inferred with controlled uncertainty. For the random transposition shuffle, a conjugate Bayesian estimator of the average displacement, computed from $m$ uniformly sampled cards, has relative uncertainty $1/\sqrt{2m}$, independent of the deck size $n$. The law of total variance exposes a second error, the floor $R_n$, which arises because the observed configuration is a single draw from equilibrium: no relative tolerance below $κ\sqrt{R_n}$ can be certified, even by a census. For the shuffle we derive $R_n=(2n^2+7)/\bigl(5(n-1)^2(n+1)\bigr)$ and show that the posterior remains calibrated. At the critical point of the two-dimensional Ising model the floor of the energy vanishes, whereas that of the magnetisation stays near $0.072$; its mean-field limit is $\sqrt{2}-1$. For correlated fields read by sensor networks, the usual 95\% interval covers the ensemble mean in 7\% of cases. Bounding the floor from one realisation, as in single-chain Monte Carlo diagnostics, requires an assumption on the decay of correlations.

</details>


### [22] [Cutting, Shuffling and Diffusion as a Coarsening Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35979)
*Daniel Lester,Tanguy LeBorgne,Joris Heyman*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究切割-混洗扩散（CSD）过程，证明其为粗化过程，特征尺度为 L(n_c·Pe/2)^(-1/3) 而非 Batchelor 尺度；切割在波数空间中表现为分数阶拉普拉斯算子，并给出精确混合速率公式 γ=2^(1/3)|a₁′|n_c^(2/3)λ·Pe^(-1/3)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切割与混洗（CS）能在无拉伸的情况下重新排列流体，但实现均匀化仍需扩散（D）参与，而 CS 与 D 的相互作用机制尚不清楚；现有 Batchelor 尺度理论无法描述这类无拉伸混合过程的特征尺度。

Abstract: Conditional mutual information (CMI) characterizes dependence between two regions conditioned on an intervening buffer. Its characteristic decay length, the Markov length, has recently been identified as a diverging length scale at mixed-state phase transitions. Here, we investigate CMI dynamics in one-dimensional classical stochastic models and propose a universal scaling form for systems whose Markov length diverges with time. We derive this form in the inactive phase of parity-conserving directed percolation, where domain-wall coarsening generates a growing length scale and a self-similar probability distribution. We introduce a Hankel-based truncation scheme that enables matrix-product-state simulations at long times and over large spatial regions. Using this method, we verify the proposed scaling both in the inactive phase and at the nonequilibrium critical point, where the dynamical critical exponent governs Markov-length growth. More broadly, the same scaling form captures cases in which collective inference sets the diverging Markov length. Despite these distinct mechanisms, the CMI between fixed microscopic regions exhibits a common scaling dimension of two. Finally, we extend this framework to temporal CMI, establishing an analogous relation between the strength of conditional dependence in time domain and its characteristic memory time.

Abstract: Swarmalators are particles which have internal degrees of freedom in the form of phase oscillators that can synchronize and change the dynamics of the particles. We explore the active matter disk swarmalator model proposed by Adorjáni [Phys. Rev. E 109, 024607 (2024)] where there is an additional attraction or repulsion determined by whether the phases of particles within a certain radius synchronize or anti-synchronize. This model in the absence of swarmalator rules forms a motility-induced phase-separated (MIPS) state at high activity, but when the swarmalator rules are present, a number of additional states appear including an active gel, labyrinth state, frustrated MIPS, and frustrated fluid states. Here we examine ratchet effects for the Adorjáni model coupled to asymmetric substrates. We find that swarmalator interactions can strongly enhance or suppress active matter ratcheting depending on the structural state of the system, and show that the ratchet efficiency depends on the substrate strength, substrate lattice spacing, and the motor force on the particles. The gel state exhibits the weakest ratcheting for small substrate spacing and can even produce a reversed ratchet motion, but shows the strongest ratcheting for large motor forces. The labyrinth state exhibits the strongest ratcheting over a wide range of parameters since the quasi-one-dimensional labyrinth morphology can match the substrate geometry well.

Abstract: We apply the Constructive Cavity Method (CCM) to the dense, Ising type, restricted and deep Boltzmann machine with Gaussian couplings between adjacent layers. After identifying the core and interface contributions, CCM identifies the blanket decomposition of the cavity variables with the Aizenman--Sims--Starr random overlap structure. We apply the Random Energy Model (REM) universality ansatz to represent the cavity free energies by those of a generalized random energy model (GREM) and evaluate them recursively through Poisson point processes (PPPs). In the layer-wise gauge each layer is cut with its own cumulative fraction $|U_\ell\cap L_p|=λ_p q_\ell^{(p)}N+o(N)$, with $\mathbf 0=\mathbf q_0\le\mathbf q_1\le\cdots\le\mathbf q_r=\mathbf 1$, where $\mathbf q_\ell=(q_\ell^{(1)},\ldots,q_\ell^{(K)})$. Here $U_\ell$ is the cumulative blanket union up to blanket $\ell$. The index $p$ labels architectural layers, whereas $\ell$ labels the blankets. The dependence on the multilayer geometry is carried by the two matrices $M=2M_0Λ$ and $M_1=ΛM_0Λ$. The constructive cavity field in layer $p$ has blanket variances $Q_\ell^{(p)}-Q_{\ell-1}^{(p)}=(M(\mathbf q_\ell-\mathbf q_{\ell-1}))_p$, while the correction field has blanket variances $Q_\ell-Q_{\ell-1}=\mathbf q_\ell^\top M_1\mathbf q_\ell-\mathbf q_{\ell-1}^\top M_1\mathbf q_{\ell-1}$. The nested REM--PPP average gives the finite-level constructive cavity functional $\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{RBM/DBM}}^{\mathrm{CCM}}(ζ,\mathbf q)=\log2+\sum_{p=1}^Kλ_pX_0^{(p)}-\frac12\sum_{\ell=0}^{r-1}ζ_\ell(\mathbf q_{\ell+1}^\top M_1\mathbf q_{\ell+1}-\mathbf q_\ell^\top M_1\mathbf q_\ell)$. The balanced scalar gauge is recovered by imposing $\mathbf q_\ell=q_\ell\mathbf 1$. Selecting the physical value in the non-convex multilayer regime is a separate problem.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We introduce and analyze the $δ$-Ising model, a kinetic Ising ferromagnet in which single-spin flips are accepted when their energy change does not exceed a tunable threshold. Varying the field and threshold opens or closes local flip channels, dividing parameter space into kinetic sectors, each governed by a fixed Markov transition operator. We prove that the dynamics retains global spin-inversion symmetry exactly when up and down spins have equal numbers of open channels. In one such sector on a periodic chain of length $L$, unbiased random initial states therefore reach the field-aligned and antifield states with probability $1/2$ each. Closing a single channel leaves both states absorbing but makes the probability of ordering against the field decay as $(2/π)^L$. Parameter sweeps correspond to ordered products of sector operators, which separate reaching a state from holding it, so hysteresis appears when a state is reached and released at different sector boundaries. We also show how increasing the system size can induce a Mpemba-like reversal, allowing a state with more misaligned spins to reach the final ordered state sooner on average. On a periodic chain, this reversal arises because the mean absorption time grows logarithmically with system size for many short domains but linearly for a single macroscopic domain, and it disappears when the bulk flip channel is opened. These findings show how a simple, intuitive rule for Ising dynamics links local kinetic constraints to field response, memory, and relaxation within a unified framework.

Abstract: Understanding the fluctuations and response of particles in potential landscapes is a fundamental problem in nonequilibrium statistical physics. Here, we investigate how negative mobility reshapes the stochastic behavior of a particle moving in a mechanical potential landscape. We consider a one-dimensional overdamped particle subject to additive noise, with mobility that is negative at weak forces and positive at strong forces. In a potential well, negative mobility destabilizes the mechanical potential minimum and produces bimodal localization at two off-center positions. The same stability reversal transforms conventional Kramers escape from a potential well into a two-stage escape process involving an intermediate metastable state. When an additional spatially uniform force is applied, the mechanical perturbation translates rather than tilts the effective double well, yielding a positive response dominated at weak noise by intrawell relaxation, even though slow interwell switching dominates spontaneous positional fluctuations. These findings reveal how negative mobility reshapes the interplay among mechanical landscapes, stochastic stability, and response.

Abstract: Interactions shape the properties of many physical and biological nonequilibrium systems, but whether collective operation raises or lowers entropy production remains difficult to predict. Here, we show that strong coupling suppresses irreversible circulation associated with the directly coupled degrees of freedom. In identical two-dimensional systems, this suppression is monotonic for nonzero coupling modes and guarantees a monotonic decrease of the entropy production rate (EPR), with the corresponding probability currents vanishing in the strong-coupling limit. In higher dimensions, suppressing the circulation associated with the coupled degrees of freedom does not generally eliminate all irreversible circulation, since the remaining degrees of freedom can still sustain nonequilibrium currents. The EPR of these residual dynamics sets the strong-coupling limit, providing a criterion for whether a given choice of coupled variables leads to lower dissipation than the uncoupled system. The approach to this limit depends on how the finite-coupling current correction aligns with the residual current. Further, we show that convergence to residual dynamics in the strong-coupling limit also applies to coupled non-identical systems. Finally, we demonstrate the predicted EPR reduction in nonlinear models of vertebrate hair-cell bundles and bacterial chemotaxis.

Abstract: In recent years, statistical physics tools have been used to study the dynamics of macroscopic quantum and classical integrable models. These studies have shown that, in the thermodynamic limit, long term time-averages of local observables equal statistical averages calculated with a Generalized Gibbs Ensemble (GGE). In the latter, the weight in phase space involves all constants of motion multiplied by Lagrange multipliers which are fixed by their initial values. We argue that the GGE can be interpreted as a proper Boltzmann measure of a different (non-standard) mechanical model. This measure can therefore be sampled with stochastic methods implemented, for example, as a Langevin process. We tailor the deterministic force in the Langevin equations so that at long times the stochastic trajectories sample the desired measure. We use the integrable Neumann Model -- the model of a classical particle constrained to move on a sphere under anisotropic harmonic potentials -- as a benchmark. We test the method in a setup where the Lagrange multipliers are fixed by the GGE governing the asymptotic dynamics of the isolated Neumann model following a quench. This problem has different phases depending on the initial conditions and quench protocol and, in all cases, with the stochastic evolution of an adequately tuned Langevin process we reproduce the asymptotic values of relevant observables.

Abstract: We construct a minimal field theory for critical quantum active matter, describing a nonconserved scalar order parameter with Landau-Ginzburg ordering and self-advection. When diffusive noise dominates, higher-order quantum vertices and non-Gaussian noise fluctuations remain relevant alongside the deterministic nonlinearities, with an upper critical dimension $d_c=2$. A one-loop renormalization-group analysis in $d=2-ε$ reveals a purely dissipative fixed point, where nonlinear noise suppresses convection, and a quantum convective saddle, where interactions, convection, and non-Gaussian noise coexist. We determine the critical exponents, show that the convective regime is distinct from conventional Burgers hydrodynamics, and discuss how it could govern transient universal scaling in microscopic models of quantum active matter before a crossover to semi-classical dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: Designing sustainable alloys must reconcile high performance with real-world constraints, including feedstock scarcity and variability, processing and energy requirements, regulatory thresholds, and economic limitations. Addressing these constraints reaches far beyond today's conventional performance-focused frameworks. It needs to consider not only complex property-processing-performance trade-offs but also sustainable production pathways. Here, we propose a physics-informed ensemble (PIE) learning framework that integrates fundamental structure-property relationships with processing routes and sustainability criteria for mechanism-based alloy design. It predicts properties from composition-microstructure relationships and infers processing routes under sustainability constraints, establishing an interdisciplinary approach to identifying sustainable alloy solutions. We validate the approach for Mg alloys, a lightweight yet energy-intensive metallic material class, considering performance, cost, and carbon emissions. Trained on a benchmark dataset, the PIE model systematically explores plausible alloy compositions and microstructures to construct a digital library of millions of candidate alloys. From this space, we identify two sustainable Mg alloy solutions that overcome the conventional performance-cost-emission trade-off: a rare-earth-containing alloy increases yield and tensile strength by 72.8 percent and 61.1 percent, respectively, over alloys from the same family at comparable cost and emissions; and a rare-earth-free alloy reduces cost and carbon emissions by 40.4 percent and 88.9 percent, respectively, relative to its primary-feedstock counterpart, without compromising mechanical performance. This work introduces a general, physics-informed, and quantifiable framework for designing sustainable alloys under real-world constraints.

</details>

Abstract: Near-surface bismuth donors are promising for silicon quantum devices because of their rich electron-nuclear spin structure and long spin coherence, but their electrical activation is strongly influenced by implantation-induced amorphization. We compare redistribution and electrical activation of 20 keV Bi implanted directly into Si and after Si preamorphization. The two routes produce amorphous-layer depths of approximately 47 nm and 177 +/- 15 nm, respectively. After annealing at 650 C for 300 s, Rutherford backscattering spectrometry and channeling reveal comparable substitutional Bi fractions of 91 +/- 2% and 89 +/- 4%, whereas Hall measurements indicate Hall-derived electrical activation yields of approximately 60% and 90%, respectively. Depth-resolved ion-scattering analysis reveals a buried Bi-related feature at or beyond the initial amorphous/crystalline interface, within the end-of-range region, together with increasing surface segregation at higher annealing temperatures. The buried feature is consistent with trapping near residual end-of-range defects. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy and low-temperature photoluminescence on the Bi-only sample reveal no resolvable Bi-oxide contribution and a donor-related optical signature, respectively. These results show that amorphization depth and regrowth geometry govern Bi redistribution and influence electrical activation beyond substitutional incorporation alone.

Magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal a direction dependence, including a broad anomaly near 50 K for selected magnetic-field orientations. However, the absence of corresponding anomalies in heat capacity, electrical resistivity, and lattice parameters suggests that this feature is more consistent with an anisotropic magnetic crossover than a bulk thermodynamic phase transition. These results highlight how chemical substitution at the f-element site can induce Al-sublattice reconstruction and modify the magnetic response within a closely related Ir-Al structural framework.

Abstract: High-throughput computational and generative approaches are proposing candidate materials far faster than they can be synthesized, making it increasingly important to identify which hypothetical materials are likely to be experimentally realizable. Positive-unlabeled (PU) learning has emerged as a representative framework for synthesizability prediction, yet existing approaches treat experimentally reported materials as a static collection, overlooking the historical trajectory of materials discovery and the diverse practical factors that have driven its progress. Here we introduce ChronoSyn, a chronology-aware machine learning model that jointly learns synthesizability and the relative reporting order of known materials through a shared composition representation. Incorporating discovery chronology provides additional supervision beyond conventional synthesizability labels, improving prediction performance and enabling ChronoSyn to identify experimentally realizable compositions more effectively than baseline models. Applied to approximately 50 million hypothetical compositions, ChronoSyn identifies chemically coherent high-scoring families that include experimentally reported compositions unseen during training. These results highlight the chronology of materials discovery as an informative learning signal and suggest that the path along which known materials were discovered can point the way to those that will be discovered in the future.

Second, in the AA gallery the minimum is a nonmagnetic $η^6$-$η^6$ sandwich that pulls the layers together. Leaving it requires opening the gallery and switching Cr to a moment of 4-5 $μ_B$, so we map the path with separately relaxed spin branches. Abstract: We present the experimental study of spin rectification in parallel and series networks of ferromagnetic conductors. The networks were composed of Ni36Fe64 elements (wires) which, when subject to ferromagnetic resonance (FMR), develop a small spin-rectification photovoltage. We found that the effect of spin rectification can be modeled as an increase in resistance of the ferromagnetic elements and closely follows the usual dependence of total network resistance in series and parallel electric circuits: the total spin-rectification photovoltage in ferromagnetic wires connected in series is equal to the algebraic sum of contributions from individual wires, while in the parallel connection one needs to add the reciprocals of the individual contributions. Our results support a simple way to control and manipulate the magnitude of spin-rectification photovoltage in multi-element spintronic devices.

Abstract: Nanoparticle self-assembly offers a powerful route to programmable functional materials, but its dynamics remain difficult to understand because they arise from multiscale, many-body, and environment-dependent interactions. Motivation: 将宏观齿轮等机械概念迁移到分子尺度，可实现纳米运动的精确控制，其灵感源于生物机器的高效性。然而，如何在固态框架中实现并调控分子级的集体旋转运动仍然是一个挑战。

Abstract: Translating macroscopic mechanical concepts such as gears to the molecular scale offers a route to precise control over nanoscale motion, inspired by the efficiency of biological machines. In this work, we explore two-dimensional interlocked linker rotations in the flexible metal-organic framework NU-2002. The volume-dependent free energy landscape is mapped using bias-deletion metadynamics to identify six characteristic phases, ranging from very closed to very open pore. We then perform unbiased molecular dynamics simulations at each phase to track rotation events and evaluate co-rotation statistics, calculate free energy barriers, and measure pairwise radial force transfer between neighbouring linkers. We show that the free energy barrier acts as a rotational-speed filter, where rare rotational transitions lead to higher speeds on average. Across the volume profile, we classify four distinct geared regimes: sterically restricted rotations at small volumes (Jammed Gear), rotational coupling extending to third neighbour linkers (First Gear), in-plane partnered linker coupling (Second Gear), and uncoupled, independent rotation at large volumes (Neutral Gear). Together, these results establish framework breathing as a molecular gearbox that switches both the speed and collective coupling of linker rotation. Framework flexibility therefore offers a handle for programming collective motion in solid-state molecular machines.

How the hole is shared depends on the functional together with the geometry: hybrids give a full, uncoupled hole at the DFT+U geometry but a partial, weakly covalent hole at their own geometry, a change of up to 0.5 e on one oxygen. Where spin spreads across the active/environment boundary, the integer spin projection of the active space becomes a choice worth up to 0.26 e, and at most 0.09 e once the two Mn neighbors are included. Across all 82 runs the embedded mean field gives the same hole (median DMRG change 0.02-0.04 e); correlation mainly adds O-O covalency. State control and geometry consistency, not the solver, dominate the uncertainty, and total energies are not converged at 30-40 orbitals. </details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，将温度梯度转化为面内各方向垂直电场的横向热电势依赖于磁性；但互易性（reciprocity）表明无磁也可实现。然而在三重或更高旋转对称轴下，这种响应在互易的弹性带输运以及理想界面的两种各向同性相复合材料中均会消失。因此需要探究是否存在无需磁性且满足高对称性的横向热电机制，以及最少需要多少相。

Conclusion: 三相各向同性复合材料可在无需磁性的情况下、于高旋转对称条件下产生横向热电势，三相为最低相数要求。该发现为设计新型横向热电材料与器件提供了理论依据与可打印的实现路径。

Here we show that composites of three isotropic phases carry it, and that under such an axis three is the minimum. Treating the phases as colours identifies the arrangements that allow the response, and crystal nets from reticular chemistry supply printable three-dimensional ones. Recent advances have revealed rich and tunable excitonic phenomena in moiré superlattices, yet the microscopic mechanisms underlying the coupling between moiré excitons and moiré phonons remain elusive. Furthermore, we uncover a general real-space selection rule: the spatial matching of a moiré exciton's electron and hole densities with the moiré-phonon-induced potential perturbations determines their individual coupling contributions, while coherent interference between the electron and hole contributions controls the total ExPC. We demonstrate this rule in prototypical WSe2/WS2 moiré heterobilayers, revealing pronounced exciton- and phonon-mode-dependent coupling, including nearly complete suppression of ExPC through destructive electron-hole interference. These findings establish the internal electron-hole spatial configuration of moiré excitons as a key degree of freedom governing the strength and mode selectivity of their coupling to moiré phonons, opening opportunities to engineer ExPC through spatially tailored moiré potentials.

These metrics depend on the full battery stack and on the interactions among its components rather than on the active material alone. Here, we extend that framework to multi-horizon prediction by predicting a property at every cycle of a discharge trajectory rather than at a single cycle. Our multi-fidelity and Multi-horizon Deep Operator Network learns DFT redox potentials and cycle-dependent specific-capacity trajectories within one model. We train it on complete battery-stack representations and usage conditions, so it learns battery-stack interactions rather than cathode-only effects. This framework supports long-term property prediction, inverse design, and process optimization for organic battery systems.

Conclusion: ARPES 领域通行的二维强度分布加高对称方向能带比对的简化做法并不可靠，研究者应在数据采集和数据分析阶段采取更审慎、更完整的处理方式，以避免错误解读实验结果。

Abstract: Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) was developed in the 1970s-1990s as a momentum-resolved probe of the electronic structure of solids, with its basic principles summarized in early seminal works and classic reviews. The basic ARPES result is currently almost exclusively associated with the two-dimensional intensity distribution with coordinate axes labeled as Binding Energy and Momentum. Such datasets are usually directly compared to the band structure calculations along high symmetry directions of the Brillouin zone. Here we show that this is the result of an oversimplified treatment of the ARPES data leading to incorrect interpretations of the experimental results. Abstract: In non-centrosymmetric bulk materials, electrons flowing in the opposite directions often exhibit different properties. This diode-like effect (i.e., non-reciprocal electron transport) typically originates from relativistic spin-orbit coupling, and its amplitude scales with external magnetic field or magnetization in ferromagnets. Although such rectification is usually absent in antiferromagnets at zero field, recent theories predict that non-coplanar antiferromagnetic order can host large non-reciprocity even without spin-orbit coupling, magnetization or external magnetic fields. This phenomenon - the spontaneous topological diode effect - can potentially be used for efficient electrical readout of antiferromagnetic domains, while it has not been verified for real materials. Here, we report the observation of this effect in a chiral-lattice non-coplanar antiferromagnet CoTa3S6. Nonlinear resistivity measurements reveal the non reciprocal nature of electron transport along the [001] direction at zero field. The observed non-reciprocity exceeds that in typical ferromagnets, whose amplitude is governed by antiferromagnetic order parameter rather than magnetization. First-principles calculations show that the non-coplanar antiferromagnetic order induces asymmetric band dispersions even without spin-orbit coupling, establishing the non-relativistic origin of the observed response. The present findings demonstrate a new mechanism for efficient electrical readout of antiferromagnetic domains, and highlight non-coplanar antiferromagnets as a promising material platform for realizing ferromagnet-like functional responses without relying on spin-orbit coupling or magnetization.

Abstract: Opening a band gap in metallic two-dimensional carbon allotropes is an important step toward their use in electronic and optoelectronic devices, and full hydrogenation has proven to be an effective route to achieve it. Here, we propose irida-graphane, the fully hydrogenated derivative of the irida-graphene, a monolayer composed of three-, six-, and eight-membered rings, and investigate its structural, mechanical, electronic, and optical properties using density functional theory (DFT) at the PBE and HSE06 levels combined with phonon and \textit{ab initio} molecular dynamics (AIMD) simulations. The hydrogenation energy of $-0.53$ eV/H indicates that the hydrogenation of irida-graphene from molecular hydrogen is exothermic. The absence of imaginary phonon frequencies confirms the dynamical stability of irida-graphane, and AIMD simulations show that its structure remains intact at temperatures up to 2000 K. Irida-graphane is mechanically isotropic, with an in-plane stiffness of approximately 156 N/m, a Poisson's ratio of approximately 0.1, and critical strains of approximately 20%. Hydrogenation transforms the metallic parent structure into a semiconductor with an indirect band gap of 4.68 eV (PBE) and 6.0 eV (HSE06). Irida-graphane is transparent in the visible range, with reflectivity below 0.3%, and absorbs strongly in the deep ultraviolet, with absorption coefficients of the order of $10^{5} - 10^{6}$ cm$^{-1}$. These results suggest irida-graphane as a candidate wide-gap material for ultraviolet optoelectronics and transparent coatings.

Motivation: 磁性材料设计需要同时生成晶体结构及其位点分辨的磁矩，且需定向探索具有特定化学组成和磁性能的新材料；同时发现"筛选通过率更高"并不必然带来更多化学式新颖的合格候选，重复生成相同化学式会限制候选多样性，因此需要研究采样策略与模型设计对生成质量和多样性的影响。

Result: 系统实验表明各设计因素（编码器/解码器、磁矩表示、采样策略）显著影响重构与生成质量/多样性；验证了重复生成相同化学式会限制合格候选的累计。模型在两次各一千万次的采样中运行，识别出化学式不在参考数据集内、形成能为负且估算饱和磁化强度高的含 Nd 候选材料。

Conclusion: GMMag 能够有效联合生成晶体结构与位点分辨磁矩，并通过高斯混合采样实现无重训练的目标定向生成；成功产出具有新化学式和理想磁性能的候选材料，为磁性晶体材料的逆向设计提供了可行工作流。

Abstract: We introduce GMMag, a workflow for generating crystal structures together with their site-resolved magnetic moments, built on a conditional variational autoencoder with a graph neural network encoder and a transformer decoder. Gaussian-mixture sampling redirects generation towards selected chemical and magnetic targets without retraining. We systematically investigate how encoder and decoder design, the representation of magnetic-moment magnitudes and relative signs, and sampling choices affect reconstruction, generation quality and diversity. Because a higher screening pass rate does not necessarily yield more qualifying candidates with distinct chemical formulas absent from the reference dataset, we model how repeated generation of the same chemical formulas limits their accumulation and test the model in two separate ten-million-draw runs. As an application, the workflow identifies Nd-containing candidates with chemical formulas absent from the reference dataset, negative formation energies and high estimated saturation magnetization.

</details>

Abstract: Magnetic textures offer a route toward photonic functionality in which electromagnetic fields are controlled not only by material composition and geometry but also by the internal magnetic configuration. Conversely, spatial texturing of these fields is a qualitatively new tool to increase the effective coupling strength to magnetic matter and to act on the the vortex topology and magnetic dynamics. Here, we investigate the coupling and scattering of structured electromagnetic fields by a magnetic vortex, focusing on how spatially non-collinear magnetization governs polarization conversion, electromagnetic energy flow, and magnetization dynamics. We develop a spherical rigorous coupled-wave method that incorporates the spatially dependent anisotropic magnetic permeability associated with the vortex texture. The calculations show that the off-diagonal components of the magnetic susceptibility mediate conversion into complementary polarization channels that are absent from the incident field. The generated fields exhibit pronounced spatial localization associated with the vortex magnetization profile and support a nonzero azimuthal component of electromagnetic energy flow. A comparison between linearly and radially polarized illumination reveals an approximately one-order-of-magnitude difference in the scattered fields amplitudes due to polarization texturing, demonstrating the importance of spatial overlap between the incident field and the anisotropic magnetic response. The back-action on the magnetic vortex dynamics is quantitatively demonstrated by numerical simulations for the inverse Faraday effect due to the rectification of structured effective-field excitation. It is shown that polarization textured optical fields can drive vortex-core precession and, at sufficiently strong excitation, reverse the core polarity and chirality on nanosecond time scale.

Abstract: Conventional altermagnetic spin splitting arises from staggered exchange and sublattice-dependent anisotropic hopping without requiring spin-orbit coupling (SOC). Here we derive a low-energy effective model showing that SOC and staggered exchange can jointly activate additional spin splitting on momentum planes where the nonrelativistic contribution vanishes. In hexagonal $α$-MnTe, this SOC-activated contribution produces d-wave spin splitting near $Γ$ with out-of-plane spin polarization. Its leading angular pattern counter-rotates by half the in-plane Néel-vector rotation angle. First-principles spin-transport calculations reveal a corresponding approximate half-angle locking between the Néel-vector orientation and the electric-field directions maximizing the signed longitudinal and transverse spin conductivities. Analytical transport calculations based on the effective model trace this locking to the same angular coupling between momentum and the Néel vector that governs the spin-splitting pattern. Our findings connect the microscopic mechanism of SOC-activated altermagnetic spin splitting to directional spin transport, providing a theoretical basis for probing Néel-vector orientation and controlling spin-current generation.

Abstract: Functionalising the back-end-of-line (BEOL) of CMOS with emerging materials is a key route to neural network accelerators and neuromorphic systems. In this work, we demonstrate for the first time the integration of 2-terminal ferroelectric synaptic weights in the BEOL of as-delivered commercial 180 nm CMOS dies. A novel material stack is proposed, in which a WOx/[HfO2-ZrO2] bilayer is sandwiched between TiN and Mo electrodes. For devices annealed at 450 °C, programming below 1.8 V is achieved with a cycle-to-cycle variation below 3 %. Synaptic weights annealed at 500 °C exhibit more gradual weight updates, while the tested underlying NMOS transistors remain functional after the complete integration process. The trade-off between thermal budget and on-device training performance is illustrated using hardware-aware neural network simulations.

Abstract: Polymorphism in molecular crystals presents a major challenge for controlling crystallization. Here, we combine experiment and theory to characterize the polymorph landscape of acridine and investigate its selective control by additives. Melt crystallization experiments resolve the previously unknown structure of form VIII, while topological crystal structure prediction recovers all seven known unsolvated polymorphs with determined crystal structures. Combining a fine-tuned machine-learned interatomic potential with anharmonic free energy calculations reproduces the experimentally observed stability relationships and reveals a shallow free energy landscape. Finally, experimentally observed additive-induced polymorph preferences correlate with mixed-crystal substitutional defect energies and topological similarities between additive and acridine crystal structures. Together, these results provide a comprehensive picture of acridine polymorphism, linking crystal structure, thermodynamic stability, and additive-controlled crystallization.

</details>

### [69] [An exactly solvable obstructed seam on a lattice Klein bottle: bulk-locked Majorana rings and register-dependent toric codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35931)
*Junhao,Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在闭格点克莱因瓶上通过字面反射粘合Wen plaquette模型，构造并精确求解了一个受挫的接缝(seam)。接缝算符在偶数周长下沿两个环反交换、在奇数周长下沿一个加倍长度的环反交换，其代数约化为自由Majorana环，而可交换的体内稳定子通过“体扇区锁定”约束允许的环扇区，从而给出严格的基态能量、激发能隙与简并度，并经相位感知Pauli迹与有限尺寸对角化（含两个24比特能量预言）验证。作者证明接缝简并非拓扑的，并进一步研究边缘寄存器、Hada-mard装扮的位点寄存器所保留的任意子标签与输运、逻辑门与码距、以及手征物质对全纯双重态的电场劈裂与两条竞争隧穿路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图厘清挫败/受阻碍的粘合(defect/obstructed gluing)与一致拓扑码实现(consistent toric-code realization)之间的关系，特别是字面反射粘合产生的接缝是否具有拓扑保护，以及由此得到的接缝代数、能谱和寄存器/逻辑门性质如何在有限尺寸系统中体现并区别于真正的拓扑码。

Method: 1) 在闭格点克莱因瓶上对Wen plaquette模型做字面反射粘合，分析接缝算符的对易/反对易关系及其在偶、奇周长下的环结构；2) 将接缝代数约化为自由Majorana环，并用可交换体内稳定子实施“体内扇区锁定”以约束环扇区；3) 用相位感知Pauli迹与有限尺寸对角化数值检验基态能量、激发能隙与简并度；4) 构造边缘寄存器与Hadamard装扮的位点寄存器，研究任意子标签保持、颜色依赖的接缝输运及简并度；5) 对枚举的格点等距与尺寸计算逻辑门与码距，区分抽象比特重标记与局部硬件深度；6) 在3个16/24比特格点上引入手征物质，考察全纯双重态及其电场劈裂的两条竞争隧穿路径。

Result: 得到受挫接缝的精确解：接缝算符在偶数周长下沿两个环反交换、奇数周长下沿一个加倍长度的环反交换；代数归为自由Majorana环，体内扇区锁定导致严格的基态能量、激发能隙和简并度。数值（相位感知Pauli迹与有限尺寸对角化，含两个24比特能量预言）与解析一致。证明接缝简并非拓扑的：被测试的单点接缝场在二阶将其劈裂。寄存器结果：边缘寄存器保留任意子标签；Hadamard装扮的位点寄存器具有颜色依赖的接缝输运，偶数周长下基态简并度为2或4，奇数周长下出现符号挫败的例外。给出各枚举等距与尺寸的逻辑门与码距。手征物质选择全纯双重态，其电场劈裂遵循两条竞争隧穿路径，但该物质结果仍是有限尺寸结论。

Conclusion: 主要成果是带有体内扇区锁定的受阻碍接缝(obstructed seam)精确解；寄存器、逻辑门和物质计算旨在界定该解与拓扑码的关系。接缝简并缺乏拓扑保护，与一致toric-code实现形成对比；手征物质结果限于有限尺寸。

Abstract: We study a frustrated seam produced by literal reflection gluing of Wen's plaquette model on a closed lattice Klein bottle. The seam operators anticommute along two cycles for even circumference and one cycle of twice the length for odd circumference. Their algebra reduces to free Majorana rings, while the commuting bulk stabilizers constrain the allowed ring sectors. This bulk locking yields exact ground energies, excitation gaps and degeneracies, checked by phase-aware Pauli traces and finite-size diagonalization, including two 24-qubit energy predictions. The seam degeneracy is not topological: the single-site seam fields tested split it at second order. We contrast this obstructed gluing with consistent toric-code realizations. An edge register preserves the anyon labels; a Hadamard-dressed site register has colour-dependent seam transport, with ground-state degeneracy 2 or 4 for even circumference and a sign-frustrated exception at odd circumference. For the enumerated lattice isometries and sizes, we compute logical gates and distances, distinguishing abstract qubit relabelling from local hardware depth. Finally, on three 16- and 24-qubit lattices, chiral matter selects a holonomy doublet whose electric-field splitting follows two competing tunnelling routes. These matter results remain finite-size statements. The main result is the exact obstructed-seam solution with bulk sector locking; the register, gate and matter calculations delimit its relation to topological codes.

</details>


### [70] [Agentic Swarms Discover Enhanchment of Fractional Chern Insulator Gaps Beyond Ideal Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35934)
*Yitong Zhang,Changwen Xu,Andrew Boshi Li,Siddhartha Sarkar,Kai Sun,Venkatasubramanian Viswanathan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出MOSAIC多智能体协作系统（Claude Opus 5.5），并借助前沿模型（GPT-6 Astra）提出理论框架，探索分数量子拓扑序的能隙优化问题。核心发现：偏离理想量子几何可大幅增强多体能隙（提升一个数量级以上），且实空间量子度量（而非动量空间）是决定多体能隙的关键量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI能否从解决良定义问题跨越到开放式的物理学发现，具体针对一个长期瓶颈：小能隙限制了分数拓扑序的鲁棒性以及任意子在更高温度下的研究难度。

Method: 采用MOSAIC多智能体协作群（Claude Opus 5.5），让前沿模型（GPT-6 Astra）自主提出理论框架，而非预设模型；多个智能体以去中心化方式并行探索、跨智能体整合结果，并进行AI辅助的理论分析与简化建模。

Result: 发现偏离理想量子几何可使多体能隙比理想情形大一个数量级以上；AI理论分析给出单带近似下无界（unbounded）的解析能隙增强因子；并识别实空间量子度量作为决定多体能隙的关键量，该指标可仅由单粒子波函数计算，无需多体精确对角化（ED）即可指导能隙优化。

Conclusion: 研究表明协作式AI智能体能够整合探索、意外物理发现与理论理解，为AI驱动的基础物理研究开辟了新可能。

Abstract: Can artificial intelligence (AI) move beyond solving well-defined problems to contribute to open-ended discovery in fundamental physics? Could AI propose what to explore, identify unexpected phenomena, and uncover their mechanisms? We address this using MOSAIC (Multi-agent Open-ended Scientific Analysis and Investigation through Collaboration), a collaborative swarm of AI agents (Claude Opus 5.5), to tackle a longstanding bottleneck: the small energy gaps limiting the robustness of fractional topological order and the study of anyons at higher temperatures. Rather than prescribing a model, we ask frontier models (GPT-6 Astra) to propose a theoretical framework, which MOSAIC explores through diverse strategies and rapid exchange of ideas. Without centralized coordination, the agents explored autonomously, combined findings across agents, and formed insights from individual runs, outperforming isolated agents with the same tools. AI-assisted theoretical analysis further develops simplified models with an analytical gap enhancement factor that is unbounded within the single-band approximation. Remarkably, AI identifies the real-space quantum metric, rather than the momentum-space one, as the key quantity governing the many-body gap, revealing how flat-band wavefunctions shape two-electron interactions and yielding an index computable from single-particle wavefunctions without many-body ED to guide gap optimization. Abstract: We present AlgebrasTQD, an open-source code written in GAP (Groups, Algorithms, and Programming), for computing condensable algebras and the corresponding topological boundaries and interfaces of twisted quantum doubles $D^ω(G)$. The functions in AlgebrasTQD compute all condensable algebras $A(H,F,γ,ε)$ with their algebra structure and anyons, sort them into isomorphism classes and identify the twin algebras that are inequivalent but share the same anyon decomposition. AlgebrasTQD also determines all the subalgebra relations, the corresponding Hasse diagram and the types of 1+1d phases for any chosen, possibly non-invertible, group-theoretical symmetry. For non-maximal algebras, the code computes the reduced topological order with its $3$-cocycle and the map of anyons across the topological interface. Finally, AlgebrasTQD includes functions that construct certain non-Clifford logical gates via non-Abelian surface codes. We summarize the formulas that are implemented in the AlgebrasTQD code, and we illustrate its use with Abelian and non-Abelian groups including $\mathbb{Z}_2^3$ with $ω_\text{III}$, $D_8$, $A_4$ with and without anomaly, twin algebras and twin phases, and the Clifford-hierarchy gates in quantum doubles of dihedral groups. All arithmetic is exact and AlgebrasTQD can be used with any finite group $G$ and 3-cocycle $ω$, limited only by the time and size of the calculation. Abstract: We investigate the interplay between confinement and Krylov complexity in the long-range transverse-field Ising chain, where power-law interactions $\sim 1/r^α$ generate an effective confining potential between domain walls without an explicit symmetry-breaking field. Using the time-averaged Krylov state complexity after a quench as a diagnostic, we find that the complexity is strongly suppressed and only weakly system size-dependent at small $α$ and small transverse field $h_x$, where confinement is strongest, and crosses over to rapid growth at larger $α$, where it increases with system size. Within the confined regime, the oscillation frequencies of the complexity agree with the meson masses of an effective two-kink model, with the dominant frequency growing linearly with the lightest meson mass and the time-averaged complexity decreasing approximately as its inverse square. At low temperature, it exhibits bounded oscillations that directly encode the meson spectrum, whereas this structure is progressively washed out as the temperature increases. Within the present DFT+U+SOC description, the spectral response therefore depends on the separate Coulomb and exchange interactions and cannot be represented completely by a single effective U-J parameter. Abstract: Super-Bloch oscillations (SBOs) offer a powerful route to control wave packet propagation in driven lattices, yet their realization in magnonic systems has remained elusive. Abstract: In rare-earth compounds, the interplay between geometric frustration and unusual single-ion anisotropy caused by the crystal electric field often leads to exotic magnetic phases. We show that the ratio of the equilibrium moment to the response extracted near zero bias equals, up to the sign, twice the bias at which the contrast changes sign, independently of the relative strength of the two mechanisms, so that this ratio itself does not measure the strength of the magneto-electric effect. We find that within the experimental energy window $|eV|\lesssim10$~meV the {bias-dependent} field-induced contribution is at most a few percent of the equilibrium one, and the two become comparable only at biases of order $100$~meV or higher. Abstract: Classical Coulomb crystals and quantum Wigner crystals typically arise when repulsive interactions order like-charged particles in spatially confined environments. Experimentally, we use a transmission electron microscope to measure number-resolved energy correlations in photoemitted pulses containing up to nine electrons, revealing strong correlations at small particle numbers that progressively disappear in the pulse interior as the number increases. To explain these observations, we combine many-body simulations with a one-dimensional statistical-mechanical model, showing that the leading and trailing electrons generate a self-induced longitudinal potential that transiently confines the inner particles, enabling the formation of an ordered chain during propagation. The resulting crystallization phase diagram reveals how many-body ordering dynamics can be understood in terms of evolving macroscopic beam properties, namely its geometry and the ratio of the mean Coulomb interaction energy to the longitudinal thermal energy. Extending this framework, we theoretically demonstrate that weak transverse guiding through electrostatic or optical ponderomotive forces can sustain crystallization at larger particle numbers and predict spontaneous longitudinal periodic bunching in continuous electron beams. Our results identify conditions under which stochastic Coulomb interactions can be harnessed to generate self-structured, strongly correlated electron beams in both pulsed and continuous regimes.

</details>

*Boyuan Shi*

TL;DR: 本论文发展了针对相互作用晶格费米子的热力学量与子系统量的图解法，重点提出逆味展开、组合求和与行列式-多项式筛选，并将其用于Hubbard模型密度及第二Rényi熵的高阶图解计算，为量子模拟器与多体近似提供基准参照。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 所发展的逆味展开、组合求和与行列式-多项式筛选方法能够高效、稳定地计算相互作用晶格费米子的高阶图解，为量子模拟器和多体近似提供可靠的数值基准，并成功将第二Rényi熵计算扩展到九至十阶及大晶格规模。

Abstract: This thesis develops diagrammatic methods for thermodynamic and subsystem quantities of interacting lattice fermions, providing reference calculations for quantum simulators and many-body approximations. I formulate an inverse-flavour expansion about a self-consistent Hartree saddle, enumerate non-Gaussian diagrams, and use a discrete Lehmann representation to integrate selected internal vertices analytically. Density results cover 31 state points of the six-flavour Hubbard model on a $70\times70$ lattice at $T/t=0.15$.

A determinant-polynomial sieve evaluates the same sums through local coefficient filters; at ninth order it is 24--25 times faster than the specified CoS baselines in three-flavour dense-random-matrix benchmarks. Thermodynamic applications include a 2048-site honeycomb lattice and a three-flavour square lattice with 1024 sites.
  For the second Renyi entropy, I derive a fermionic replica path integral and a stable evaluation of its two-time Green functions. The connected-determinant calculation reaches tenth order on a $3\times3$ cluster and ninth order on larger lattices. On $50\times50$ sites at $(U/t,μ/t,βt)=(3,1,8)$, the ninth-order continuation at shift $α=1.3$ gives $S_2/N_s=0.06695$ with propagated Monte Carlo uncertainty $3.0\times10^{-5}$. Comparing two expansion shifts at their respective retained orders gives an endpoint difference of $2.6\times10^{-4}$.

Abstract: A transition between an integer quantum Hall insulator and a chiral spin liquid can become critical without closing the single-electron gap, raising the question of which composite excitations govern the change in topological order. We investigate this question in the \(\mathrm{SU}(3)\) triangular-lattice Hofstadter--Hubbard model at one particle per site and magnetic flux \(π/3\) per triangle. Using infinite density matrix renormalization group calculations, we identify a weak-coupling integer quantum Hall phase and an interaction-driven \(\mathrm{SU}(3)_1\) chiral spin liquid. Near the transition, both the neutral and charge-\(3e\) correlation lengths increase substantially, whereas the single-electron correlation length remains short, providing evidence for a Chern--Simons--Higgs critical theory, where a gauge-invariant monopole carries the quantum numbers of a fermionic \(\mathrm{SU}(3)\)-singlet trion. We discuss the implications for doping the system and recent experiments on doping \(ν=-2/3\) fractional quantum anomalous Hall in tMoTe\(_2\).

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅由交换作用给出的电子气局域场因子在略低于 2kF 处出现尖锐峰，但量子蒙特卡洛模拟并未发现此峰，说明交换近似存在缺陷，需要更精确的理论来解释这一矛盾。

Result: 平行自旋关联表现为交换项的负像，并消去约一半的 2kF 以下尖锐峰；所计算的自旋分辨局域场因子能够复现 rs=1 和 2 时的 QMC 数据，并在 q=0 处与从基态能量得到的压缩率和自旋磁化率一致。

Conclusion: 必须同时纳入 Hedin 顶点方程核的两类（两项）图——尤其是通常被省略的第二项——才能正确描述电子气的自旋分辨局域场因子，消除交换近似引入的虚假尖锐峰，并与 QMC 及热力学量（压缩率、自旋磁化率）相符。

Abstract: Exchange alone gives the electron gas a local field factor with a sharp peak just below 2kF. Quantum Monte Carlo finds none. Both spin channels are computed here from their lowest diagrams with a dynamically screened interaction and no fitting. Parallel spin correlation is the negative image of exchange and removes about half the peak; the calculated spin-resolved factors account for the QMC data at rs = 1 and 2, and at q = 0 for the compressibility and spin susceptibility obtained from the ground-state energy. The two classes of diagrams are the two terms of the kernel of Hedin's vertex equation, of which the second is routinely dropped.

</details>

Abstract: We establish an experimentally accessible route to identifying Majorana surface states through their intrinsic Ising spin response, using UTe$_2$ as a concrete platform. The key is to extend the Majorana correlations into an adjacent metallic film via the superconducting proximity effect, thereby making the otherwise surface-localized response accessible to NMR measurements. Remarkably, disorder further sharpens the Majorana signature: impurity scattering suppresses the conventional $p$-wave proximity correlations while preserving the Majorana contribution, yielding a giant Ising-anisotropic enhancement of the NMR relaxation rate. This approach also offers a powerful means to distinguish among candidate pairing symmetries.

</details>

Result: 得到了不同纵横比下形状依赖的迈斯纳响应与有效退磁因子，并给出了小λ情形下有效样品尺寸的改进解析表达式，为从体积排斥磁化率测量精确求λ提供了高精度流程。

Conclusion: 该有限元方法为基于体积排斥的磁化率测量确定λ提供了高精度程序，可直接应用于新型超导材料的表征。

Abstract: The magnetic penetration depth, $λ$, is a fundamental parameter of the superconducting state and is commonly determined from precision measurements of the magnetic susceptibility, $χ$, in the Meissner state. However, extracting $λ$ from such measurements requires an accurate treatment of geometry-dependent demagnetization effects, for which analytic results are only available in a limited number of cases. Here we present a finite-element analysis of the magnetic response of three dimensional superconducting spheroids and cylinders with finite penetration depth. By calculating the magnetic moment and susceptibility, $χ(λ)$, over a wide range of aspect ratios, we quantify the shape-dependent Meissner response and extract effective demagnetization factors in the $λ\rightarrow 0$ limit. For small but finite $λ$, we further determine effective sample dimensions from the initial variation of $χ(λ)$ and propose an improved analytical expression for these. Our results provide a high-precision procedure for determining $λ$ from volume-exclusion based susceptibility measurements and should be directly applicable to the characterization of novel superconducting materials.

</details>

Abstract: The superconducting diode effect (SDE) is characterized by unequal critical current magnitudes for opposite-polarity current flow, owing to broken spatial-inversion and time-reversal symmetries. For a superconducting pair wavefunction to spontaneously break inversion symmetry implies the formation of Cooper pairs with nonzero momentum, yielding a nonuniform superconducting state. For singlet superconductors, the formation of such a (FFLO) state has long been understood to provide an escape from high field Pauli pair-breaking -- how and why such an effect might occur for equal spin pairing is an open question. Here we report the observation of a large SDE in bulk specimens of the three-dimensional heavy-fermion triplet superconductor UTe$_2$, with a peak diode efficiency of 33% at the high magnetic field strength of 33.6 T, representing the highest-field observation of the SDE to date. Crucially, this nonreciprocal transport is strictly confined to a narrow field regime where superconductivity coexists with a field-induced spin-polarized state near a first-order metamagnetic phase boundary. The absence of the SDE away from this slender phase-intersection argues strongly against purely extrinsic vortex pinning asymmetries. Instead, our findings indicate spontaneous inversion symmetry breaking accompanied by finite-momentum Cooper pairing. We model this behavior using a phenomenological Ginzburg-Landau theory, demonstrating that the intertwining of magnetic and triplet superconducting orders can stabilize an odd-parity FFLO state.

</details>

Abstract: Unconventional superconductors with a two-component order parameter support the existence of a rotational symmetry breaking nematic phase and a time-reversal symmetry breaking chiral phase. A recent experiment on 4Hb-TaS$_2$ surprisingly shows both twofold anisotropy in $H_{c2}$, which suggests nematic superconductivity, as well as isotropic vortices at low temperatures, which suggest chiral superconductivity. To resolve this apparent conundrum we write down the appropriate Ginzburg-Landau equations and develop a numerically efficient algorithm for finding vortex solutions, allowing us to study their shape and anisotropy over a broad parameter range. We also demonstrate analytically that, to leading order, the vortex anisotropy is independent of strain in the nematic case, but is linearly dependent on it in the chiral phase, providing a possible way to distinguish the two phases. Using all these results we show that, in the presence of strain, in a large parameter regime the system behaves nematically near the transition, leading to an anisotropy of $H_{c2}$, but is dominantly chiral away from the transition, leading to parametrically smaller vortex anisotropy, in line with the experiment.

</details>
