By employing this method, we successfully obtained samples characterized by well-defined precipitates of niobium, featuring regular sizes and grain spacing. The goal was to establish a systematic and robust method approach for obtaining samples with different of sub-micrometric precipitates distributions but also to investigate their superconducting properties. Structural characterization by optical and Scanning Electron Microscopy, dc Mdc[H,T] ac inductive , as well the magneto-transport measures rhodc[H,T], Rdc[V,I.H,T], I[V,H,T] and [H,T,I] were done. Also, using a micro Hall sensor with 10 measuring transverse voltage bridges regularly distributed, we were able to obtain the real external applied magnetic field value inside of the chamber sample space. Thus e studied the dependence of configuration of the Nb grains in the superconducting coupling order parameter properties that connects the grains superconductors to the normal Cu matrix. We verify through magnetization and transport measures the signature of the double transition. This, when we apply current of different intensities, in the transport measures, and different field values, in the inductive measures. This behavior is an indication that our system is made up of by a collection of superconducting grains immersed in a well-organized in a non-superconducting matrix. The results indicate that the superconducting properties of these systems are manifested usually through two transition temperatures Tc(H) e TcJ(H).

Abstract: Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x (Bi-2212) is a d-wave superconductor with unconventional properties intrinsic to the material itself. These properties are of growing interest for quantum technologies in electronics and photonics, and twisted Josephson junctions can make direct use of them. However, Bi-2212 is strongly anisotropic, and it is challenging to find its crystal axes in flakes in advance, which limits device engineering. Here, we show that polarized resonant Raman spectroscopy reveals the orientation of both the a and b axes of Bi-2212 optically and non-destructively. By correlating Laue diffraction, HAADF-STEM and polarized Raman scattering on the same bulk crystal, we assign the b axis, along which the incommensurate structural supermodulation propagates, to the intensity maximum of the 27 cm-1 supermodulation mode, which varies in counter-phase with the 118 cm-1 Bi/Sr mode, along the a axis. Multi-wavelength measurements (458, 532, 660 nm) show that resulting in-plane contrast peaks at 532 nm and nearly vanishes at 660 nm, indicating that the anisotropy originates from an electronic resonance induced by the supermodulation. Using this resonance, we determine the crystallographic axes of exfoliated flakes and access their symmetry channels, providing a rapid orientation tool for cuprate van der Waals heterostructures.

Abstract: The superconducting state of $2H-NbS{{e}_{2}}$ was studied using point contacts for two main orientations of the contact axis relative to the principal crystallographic orientations: along the $c$-axis and in the $ab$ plane. For contacts oriented in the $ab$ plane, dependencies proportional to the electron-phonon interaction function were obtained, which agree perfectly with the theoretical calculation. It was found that the features on the experimental curves associated with the electron-phonon interaction extend to energies twice the cutoff energy of the phonon spectrum and have an amplitude of the same order of magnitude throughout the entire energy range. It has been theoretically demonstrated that the features at energies exceeding the boundaries of the phonon density of states are caused by two-phonon scattering processes of non-equilibrium phonons on Andreev electrons. A criterion for the undisturbed nature of the crystal lattice in the region where gap features form has been found. Using $2H-NbS{{e}_{2}}-Cu$ heterocontacts in the superconducting state, a study of energy gaps was conducted for two orientations of the $NbS{e_2}$ crystal: along the $c$-axis and in the $ab$ plane. It was found that the superconducting energy gaps are anisotropic; specifically, three gaps were identified in the $ab$ plane, whereas only two were observed in the $c$ direction.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: LaNiGa2 has attracted considerable interest as a candidate unconventional superconductor, particularly due to its reported time-reversal symmetry breaking and nonsymmorphic crystal structure hosting symmetry-protected electronic band degeneracies. Here, we report the growth and characterization of superconducting LaNiGa2 thin films on MgO substrates by magnetron sputtering. X-ray diffraction reveals highly crystalline, (010)-oriented films with well-defined in-plane alignment and rotational twinning. Electrical transport measurements establish superconductivity with a transition temperature of approximately 1 K. Atomic-resolution transmission electron microscopy reveals local variations in crystallographic orientation and interlayer stacking, with a predominantly Cmcm structure containing minority Cmmm regions. The coexistence of these closely related structures reveals local stacking polymorphism that is difficult to distinguish using conventional X-ray diffraction. These findings establish a superconducting LaNiGa2 thin-film platform and provide new insight into the local crystal structure of a material whose electronic properties and superconducting pairing remain under active investigation.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套适用于共线与非共线磁性体系的旋转微扰理论，区分了"交换场的规定旋转"与"磁矩的约束旋转"，并据此推导能量曲率的格林函数表达式，分析非共线对相互作用、自旋螺旋、自旋波刚度以及标量海森堡交换投影等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理磁性相互作用的微扰方法常混淆对交换场的规定旋转与对磁矩的约束旋转，导致能量曲率与集体磁行为（如自旋波、交换参数提取）之间的联系不清晰；作者希望建立严格的理论框架，明确何种旋转方式与何种物理量对应，并澄清局部提取的交换系数为何未必具有可迁移的双线性意义。

Method: 构建旋转微扰理论，区分规定旋转（prescribed rotations）与约束旋转（constrained rotations）；在全交换劈裂条件下，通过格林函数推导规定场能量曲率（不依赖局域力定理），而约束磁矩曲率需引入静态磁响应并与逆静态磁化率关联。方法用于非共线对相互作用、平面与锥形自旋螺旋，并推导长波铁磁自旋波刚度及绝热亚晶格进动下的反铁磁自旋波速度。进一步考虑轨道非局域磁势和关联电子响应，并将完整横向对海森矩阵投影到有效标量海森堡交换上，量化未重构曲率，通过双二次与三自旋例子检验。

Result: 推导出规定场能量曲率的格林函数表达式（无需局域力定理）；约束磁矩曲率与逆静态磁化率相关；给出铁磁自旋波刚度与反铁磁自旋波速度的长波表达式；指出轨道非局域与关联效应对连接旋转曲率与集体磁行为提出额外要求；展示双二次与三自旋系统中局部提取的交换系数不能代表可迁移的双线性相互作用。

Conclusion: 严格区分两种旋转方式后，旋转微扰理论能自洽地描述共线与非共线磁体系的能量曲率及其与集体激发的联系；标量海森堡投影中未被重构的曲率以及多体（双二次、三自旋）贡献表明，局部拟合得到的交换系数具有有限的有效性与可迁移性。

Abstract: We develop rotational perturbation theory for magnetic interactions in collinear and noncollinear systems, distinguishing prescribed rotations of exchange fields from constrained rotations of magnetic moments. At the full exchange splitting, we derive Green-function expressions for prescribedfield energy curvatures directly from perturbation theory, without invoking the local force theorem; constrained-moment curvatures additionally require the stationary magnetic response and are related to the inverse static susceptibility. We examine noncollinear pair interactions and planar and conical spin spirals, and derive the long-wavelength ferromagnetic spin-wave stiffness and, assuming adiabatic sublattice precession, the antiferromagnetic spin-wave velocity. Orbital-nonlocal magnetic potentials and correlated electronic response reveal further requirements for connecting rotational curvatures to collective magnetic behavior. As a practical extension, we project the complete transverse pair Hessian onto an effective scalar Heisenberg exchange and quantify the unreconstructed curvature. Biquadratic and three-spin examples illustrate why locally extracted exchange coefficients need not represent transferable bilinear interactions.

</details>

Method: 计算二维手性费米子在包含Conway群的大非阿贝尔群作用下轨形的环面配分函数。该看似标准的计算在原理与实践层面均暴露出多个此前未解决的重大问题，作者详细描述了克服这些问题的方法，且这些方法具有普遍适用性。

Result: 开发并描述了克服轨形配分函数计算中多项重大障碍的通用方法与技术。但最终结果表明，作者所考察的这些特定轨形并不是拓扑模形式的周期性元。

Conclusion: 尽管本文发展的方法具有一般适用性，但其具体考察的轨形被证明并非拓扑模形式的周期性元，说明周期性元的搜寻仍需继续。

Abstract: We compute torus partition functions of orbifolds of two-dimensional chiral fermions by huge non-Abelian groups involving the Conway group, one of the sporadic finite simple groups. This seemingly standard problem turns out to contain, somewhat unexpectedly, multiple major issues hitherto unresolved, both at the level of the principle and at the level of practice. We describe in detail how they can be overcome.
  Although the methods described in this paper are generally applicable, the choice of the particular orbifolds we consider in this paper was motivated by the search for the periodicity element of topological modular forms. We will provide a brief introduction to this question, to explain why all the techniques in this paper had to be developed. Unfortunately, the orbifolds we considered turned out not to be the periodicity element.

</details>

*Ian Moult,Bo Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于热带场论（tropical field theory）的退火重要性采样框架，用于精确计算量子场论微扰级数的高阶行为，并将其应用于三维大质量 φ³ 理论，成功算到 325 圈，从而以 0.03% 精度确定瞬子作用量并提取瞬子涨落修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰论的高阶行为蕴含量子场论的非微扰信息，但由于标准费曼图展开极其复杂，在现实场论中难以企及高圈阶。作者希望借助热带曲线模空间上全局采样的新进展，克服指数型权重方差壁垒，实现对高圈微扰级数的精确数值计算。

Method: 在热带曲线模空间全局采样的基础上，引入热带场论的退火重要性采样（annealed importance sampling）框架，从而克服指数权重方差问题，支持高圈阶精确数值计算。将该方法应用于三维大质量 φ³ 理论，计算其两点函数（two-point function）直至 325 圈的微扰级数系数。

Result: 成功计算三维大质量 φ³ 理论两点函数到 325 圈的微扰级数。结果以 0.03% 精度确定了 φ³ 理论中的瞬子作用量（instanton action），验证了集体坐标（collective-coordinate）指数，并提取出单圈行列式及直到四圈的瞬子涨落修正，与直接的半经典（semiclassical）计算结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该退火重要性采样框架有效打破了高圈数值计算的壁垒，能够精确刻画量子场论微扰级数的高阶非微扰行为。方法具有通用性，可推广到多种相关场论，有助于定量探索其高圈（high loop）行为。

Abstract: The high order behavior of perturbation theory provides insight into non-perturbative phenomenon in quantum field theory. While extensively studied in simplified models, high order behavior is challenging to access in more realistic field theories due to the complexity of the standard Feynman diagrammatic expansion. Building on recent progress in global sampling on the moduli space of tropical curves, we introduce an annealed importance sampling framework for tropical field theory. Our approach overcomes the exponential weight-variance barrier, allowing precise numerical calculations to high orders. We apply our technique to compute the perturbative series for the two-point function in the massive three-dimensional $φ^3$ theory through 325 loops. These coefficients determine the instanton action in $φ^3$ theory to 0.03\% precision, confirm the collective-coordinate exponent, and extract the one-loop determinant and the instanton fluctuation corrections through four loops, in remarkable agreement with direct semiclassical calculations. Our approach is applicable to a variety of related field theories, facilitating the quantitative exploration of their high loop behavior.

</details>


### [99] [Cusp regularization with spin structures and gravitational anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36028)
*Elisa Tabor,Cynthia Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了 SL(2,Z) 黑洞族在尖点几何处的发散问题，将尖点正规化推广到带边界自旋结构的几何求和，并分析了引力反常导致的黑洞贡献相消现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SL(2,Z) 黑洞族存在一个对应尖点几何的聚点，导致对黑洞的求和发散。作者希望理解如何通过尖点正规化处理这一发散，并考察自旋结构及引力反常对发散与相消的影响。

Method: 将尖点正规化扩展到固定边界自旋结构下的几何求和；在 c_L=c_R 情形下，将尖点作为抵消项（counterterm）以消除三种偶边界自旋结构各自的首阶发散；进一步计算引力反常对应的自旋结构相关反常相，并分析其对黑洞贡献相消的作用。

Result: 当 c_L=c_R 时，加入尖点抵消项可消除三种偶边界自旋结构中每一种的首阶发散，且所需扣除量在三个扇区中相同，为玻色情形扣除量的三分之二；引力反常导致黑洞贡献之间相消，从而去除首阶尖点发散，除非在纯引力中 c_L-c_R=0,±24，或在 N=(1,1) 超引力中 c_L-c_R=0,±12。

Conclusion: 尖点正规化可有效推广到带自旋结构的求和，且引力反常引起的有组织相消显著削减了首阶尖点发散；发散仅在特定中心荷差（纯引力 0,±24；N=(1,1) 超引力 0,±12）处残留，并给出了驱动这些相消的自旋结构相关反常相。

Abstract: The family of $SL(2,\mathbb Z)$ black holes has an accumulation point corresponding to a cusped geometry, which causes the sum over black holes to diverge. We extend cusp regularization to the sum over geometries compatible with a fixed boundary spin structure. For $c_L=c_R$, including the cusp as a counterterm cancels the leading divergence for each of the three even boundary spin structures. The required subtraction is the same in all three sectors and is two thirds of the bosonic one. We further show that gravitational anomalies cause cancellations between black hole contributions, removing the leading cusp divergence except at $c_L-c_R=0,\pm24$ in pure gravity and $c_L-c_R=0,\pm12$ in $\mathcal N=(1,1)$ supergravity. We also derive the spin structure dependent anomaly phases responsible for these cancellations.

</details>


### [100] [Analytic Bootstrap Solution for Quantum Mechanics on a Circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36031)
*David Berenstein,Upamanyu Moitra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 作者将量子力学 bootstrap 方法应用于圆环上的粒子（即 Mathieu 方程/周期势问题），利用测度傅里叶系数需指数衰减的物理条件，将解表示为（广义）连分数，从而解析地给出能量本征谱，并能高效分辨能带结构中指数级窄的间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理情形下，Schrödinger 方程解的测度傅里叶系数需满足指数衰减；作者希望利用这一物理约束，从测度的递归关系直接构造解析解，避免传统数值方法在解析指数级小能隙时的困难。

Method: 采用量子力学 bootstrap 方法：写出圆环上粒子对应测度的傅里叶系数递归关系，要求系数指数衰减，从而将解化为（广义）连分数形式；该解是耦合常数与能量的解析函数。方法还可直接计算态密度（物理性判断化为对单个变量的不等式检验），重建波函数，并从能量提取准动量。

Result: 方法能解析求解圆环上的 Schrödinger 方程（Mathieu 方程为其最简单情形），可轻松分辨能带谱中指数级窄的带隙；态密度的物理性判定简化为单变量不等式，且能重建波函数并获得准动量-能量关系。

Conclusion: 量子力学 bootstrap 方法在圆环问题中给出解析的连分数解，对耦合常数和能量解析，并能高效处理指数级小能带间隙，展示了该方法在解析谱分析中的有效性。

Abstract: We solve the Schrödinger equation for a particle in the circle with a quantum mechanical bootstrap method. The main idea is that in physical situations one needs solutions of the recursion relations for the Fourier coefficients of the measure that vanish exponentially fast. We show that this results in a (generalized) continued fraction representation of the solution. The solution is an analytical function of the couplings and the energy. We also show how one this method directly computes the density of states and checking that the answer is physical is reduced to checking an inequality for a single variable. We also show how to reconstruct the wave function and how to extract the quasimomentum of the particle as a function of the energy. We illustrate our solution in the simplest case of the Mathieu equation, where we show that the method can easily resolve exponentially small bands in the band spectrum.

</details>


### [101] [Cosmology of the Baby Universe in Coupled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36032)
*Andrew Sontag*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了耦合SYK模型中婴儿宇宙的结构，通过发展有限N、p下马约拉纳费米子迹的组合论证，给出了[1]中弦规则(Chord rules)的有限λ完成。研究发现热AdS与AS²相之间存在一阶相变，该相变在β→∞极限下依然存在；随后探讨婴儿宇宙的洛伦兹时间演化，得到余弦尺度因子，证实了体宇宙学的大爆炸/大挤压性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AS²几何在耦合SYK模型实现中所产生的婴儿宇宙结构，需要超越[1]中大N弦图规则的近似，建立有限N、p且有限λ下仍然成立的精确组合工具，以理解该模型在全部参数范围内的行为。

Method: 作者开发并利用关于多个马约拉纳费米子迹的组合论证，这些论证在有限N和p下保持有效，从而为[1]中的弦规则提供了有限λ的完备化；进而用这些方法刻画由重粒子插入质量和SYK与Maldacena-Qi耦合常数之比所参数化的相结构，并分析婴儿宇宙的洛伦兹时间演化。

Result: 发现热AdS相与AS²相之间存在一阶相变，且该相变在β→∞极限下持续存在；相变由重粒子插入质量以及SYK与Maldacena-Qi耦合常数之比参数化。洛伦兹时间演化分析给出余弦形式的尺度因子，证实体宇宙学呈现大爆炸/大挤压(big bang / big crunch)特性。

Conclusion: 通过有限N、p的组合方法完成了弦规则的有限λ推广，揭示了婴儿宇宙中稳健的一阶相变，并验证了其大爆炸/大挤压的宇宙学演化，为AS²几何的量子引力实现提供了更精确的理解。

Abstract: We analyze the structure of the baby universe which arises in the coupled SYK realization of the AS$^2$ geometry. To do this, we develop and leverage combinatorial arguments about traces of many Majoranas which remain valid at finite $N$ and $p$ and thus furnish the finite $λ$ completion of the chord rules found in [1]. We find that there is a first order phase transition which persists in the $β\rightarrow\infty$ limit between the thermal AdS and AS$^2$ phases parameterized by the mass of the heavy particle insertion and the ratio between the SYK and Maldacena-Qi coupling constants. We then explore Lorentzian time evolution in the baby universe and find a cosine scale factor confirming the big bang / big crunch nature of the bulk cosmology.

</details>


### [102] [BPS Chaos and Fortuity in Higgs Branches I: The Gepner Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36035)
*Micha Berkooz,Trivko Kukolj,Ohad Mamroud,Josef Seitz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究II型弦论Calabi-Yau紧化中的BPS态，这些态可用箭图量子力学描述，其Higgs分支为完全交流形。作者将态分为可延续到近Higgs库仑分支的多中心monotone态与占绝大多数的pure-Higgs（fortuitous）态两类，对应Higgs分支靶空间上两种不同的上同调。并提出以Berry/Chern曲率作为BPS混沌的探针，用局域化技术在Gepner模型中计算了该曲率及其RMT性质与偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦论Calabi-Yau紧化中大量BPS态的简并性类似BPS黑洞，但其中绝大多数（pure-Higgs态）无法用多中心超引力构型描述，其物理本质与数学刻画尚不清晰；作者希望为这两类态给出严格的分类依据，并寻找刻画该系统BPS混沌的可计算探针。

Method: 采用箭图量子力学框架，将BPS态对应Higgs分支（完全交流形）的靶空间；将态区分为可延续到近Higgs库仑分支的monotone态与固定在特定R荷处的fortuitous（pure-Higgs）态，并将其数学意义分别识别为靶空间上的两种不同上同调。提出以Berry/Chern曲率作为BPS混沌的探针，并借助辅助二维CFT中的局域化技术，在Gepner模型中具体计算该曲率、其随机矩阵理论（RMT）性质及对RMT的偏离。

Result: 确立了BPS态分为monotone态与fortuitous（pure-Higgs）态的精确数学分类——对应两种不同上同调；指出Berry/Chern曲率是探测BPS混沌的自然探针，并在Gepner模型中得到该曲率的显式计算结果，呈现RMT型统计性质以及偏离RMT的行为。

Conclusion: Calabi-Yau紧化中BPS态可被清晰划分为对应于两类上同调的monotone态与pure-Higgs（fortuitous）态；Berry/Chern曲率可作为BPS混沌的有效探针，且在Gepner模型中的局域化计算展示了近RMT的统计特征与系统性偏差，为理解弦论BPS系统与黑洞物理的联系提供了新工具。

Abstract: The BPS states in Calabi-Yau compactifications of type II string theory can sometimes be described using quiver quantum mechanics whose Higgs branch is a complete intersection manifold. Their degeneracy resembles that of BPS black holes. A small fraction of these states corresponds to multi-center supergravity brane configurations and can be continued to the (near-Higgs) Coulomb branch. We identify them as monotone states. The overwhelming majority of states, known as pure-Higgs states, are fortuitous states, which concentrate at a particular $R$-charge. This division has a precise mathematical meaning as two different types of cohomologies of the Higgs branch target space. We argue that a natural probe of BPS chaos in these models is the Berry/Chern curvature. In the case of the Gepner model, we use localization techniques in an auxiliary two-dimensional CFT to compute the curvature, its RMT properties, and deviations from it.

</details>


### [103] [Local Measures in Odd de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36045)
*Guilherme L. Pimentel,Nicole Righi,Behrang Tafreshi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明奇数维德西特空间中的无质量理论，其树级晚时波泛函的模平方（即概率测度）是一个局域泛函：所有波函数系数在分离位置处为零，非局域奇异性只影响相位。因此可对任意点数写出封闭公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在偶数维德西特空间中，无质量理论的波泛函模平方通常是非局域的，并带有复杂奇异性，这使得对任意点数的概率分布难以写出封闭公式。作者希望揭示奇数维情形下测度具有显著简化的局域结构，从而为这类宇宙学关联函数提供可操作的解析工具。

Method: 作者从德西特空间中无质量场的树级晚时波泛函出发，分析其模平方的解析结构。核心观察是：在奇数维中，非局域波函数中常见的奇异性全部被吸收进波泛函的相位，而模平方仅保留局域部分，导致所有分离位置上的波函数系数为零。基于此，可以直接构造出任意点数的封闭公式。

Result: 结果确认奇数维德西特空间中的无质量理论在树级层次上具有局域的概率测度。作者进一步将该结论应用于三个具体例子：非线性 sigma 模型、杨-米尔斯理论与广义相对论，覆盖三维到七维，验证了任意点数下均可写出封闭公式。

Conclusion: 奇数维德西特空间为无质量理论提供了远比偶数维简单的树级测度结构，其局域性使得任意点数的波函数模平方可以解析地封闭写出，为研究德西特宇宙学关联函数提供了新的简化途径。

Abstract: We show that massless theories in odd-dimensional de Sitter space have very simple measures---the modulus squared of the late-time wavefunctional---at tree level. They are local functionals; in particular, all wavefunction coefficients vanish at separated positions. All the usual singularities of nonlocal wavefunctions in even dimensions only affect the phase in the odd dimensional case. Hence a closed formula can be written for any number of points. We focus on three examples: the non-linear sigma model, Yang-Mills theory and General Relativity, from dimensions three to seven.

</details>


### [104] [The Fate of 1/4-BPS States in the D1-D5 CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36057)
*Suzanne Bintanja,Amey P. Gaikwad,Dikshant Rathore*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 AdS3×S3×T4 的 D1-D5 系统背景下，围绕 SymN(T4) 进行共形微扰，精确到任意 N 计算低能扇区的 1/4-BPS 算符反常维度，发现反常维度形式简单且与短多重在重整化中融合为长多重复合结构一致，并验证有限 N 下一阶形变的 BPS 态密度与超引力结果相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望研究 D1-D5 系统中 1/4-BPS 算符在离开超引力点、引入微扰形变后，哪些短多重的保护态会被解除保护（获得反常维度）而融合为长多重，从而检验 BPS 态密度是否仍与超引力低能谱一致。

Method: 以 SymN(T4) 为对称积点作共形微扰论，围绕该点对 D1-D5 系统（AdS3×S3×T4）的低能扇区精确到任意 N 展开，计算对应 1/4-BPS 算符的一阶（首阶非平凡）反常维度，并据此统计 BPS 态密度。

Result: 所有得到的反常维度都与短多重融合为长多重的复合机制一致，且形式特别简单；在有限 N 与一阶形变下，所考虑扇区的 BPS 态密度与超引力点结果一致，表明并非所有可能被解除保护的态都实际被解除保护。

Conclusion: 在所考察的共形微扰阶数内，结果确认了 D1-D5 系统部分 BPS 态在有限 N 下仍保持受保护状态，验证了微扰下 BPS 谱与超引力的对应关系。

Abstract: We consider the 1/4-BPS operators in the D1-D5 system on AdS$_3\times S^3\times T^4$. We evaluate the anomalous dimensions of low-lying sectors in conformal perturbation theory around Sym$^N(T^4)$ exactly in $N$. All anomalous dimensions are consistent with recombination of the short multiplets into long ones and take a particularly simple form. We show that at finite $N$ and at first nontrivial order in the deformation, for the sectors we consider, the BPS density of states agrees with that of the supergravity point. To the order in conformal perturbation theory we consider, this confirms that not all states that can get lifted do get lifted.

</details>


### [105] [Axionic Quintessence in Heterotic String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36064)
*David Andriot,Margherita Putti,Leo Veissiere*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在杂化弦紧致化框架下研究轴子型 quintessence，从十维理论推导四维轴子有效作用量，考察超轻轴子产生的几何条件，并结合 K3 纤维化构型与宇宙学观测进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找能够解释宇宙暗能量（晚期加速膨胀）的超轻轴子候选，并检验其能否在杂化弦紧致化的现实几何中得到实现。

Method: 从十维杂化弦理论出发，推导包含规范耦合与非微扰势的四维轴子有效作用量并识别可修正它的曲率项；通过对环面卡拉比-丘超曲面的探索性扫描，考察世界面瞬子指数压低产生超轻轴子的几何条件；在 K3 纤维化实例中检验单圈修正规范动能函数正定性，并利用对准的世界面瞬子贡献缓解张力；最后定性比较所得 quintessence 势的晚期宇宙学解与观测偏好区间。

Result: 探索性扫描发现超轻轴子的产生受到显著几何限制；K3 纤维化最小基准模型与单圈修正规范动能函数的正定性存在张力，但通过对准的世界面瞬子贡献可缓解该张力，获得具有现象学相关衰变常数的超轻轴子；同时识别出必须加以控制的更低作用量瞬子贡献；所得 quintessence 势的晚期宇宙学解经定性比较处于观测偏好范围内。

Conclusion: 在杂化弦紧致化中通过世界面瞬子产生超轻轴子型 quintessence 在几何上高度受限，但在 K3 纤维化等构型中借助对准瞬子贡献可实现符合观测量级的候选，并需进一步控制低作用量瞬子以维持超轻性。

Abstract: We investigate axionic quintessence in heterotic string compactifications. Starting from the ten-dimensional theory, we derive the four-dimensional axion effective action, including gauge couplings and non-perturbative potentials, and identify the curvature terms that can correct it. We examine the geometric requirements for ultralight axions generated by exponentially suppressed worldsheet instantons, finding significant restrictions through an exploratory scan of toric Calabi-Yau hypersurfaces. In an illustrative K3-fibred setup, the minimal benchmark also encounters a tension with the positivity of the one-loop corrected gauge kinetic functions. Aligned worldsheet-instanton contributions provide a way to alleviate this tension and yield an ultralight axion with a phenomenologically relevant decay constant. Our analysis also identifies lower-action instantons whose contributions must be controlled to preserve the ultralight axion. We then study the resulting class of quintessence potentials and qualitatively compare their late-time cosmological solutions with observationally preferred ranges.

</details>


### [106] [Non-Hermitian Schwinger effect in an imaginary magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36080)
*Ahmad Jafar Arifi,Kei Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究非厄米施温格效应：在实电场与平行虚磁场背景下分析标量和狄拉克QED的一圈有效拉氏量，发现虚磁场会产生额外的极点族，且单独即可产生非零虚部；两场之间存在抵消机制，当两场强之比为有理数时需处理双重极点；并揭示了来自Landau能级的真空衰变率呈正弦式场依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索虚磁场背景（一种非厄米设置）下QED有效拉氏量的解析结构，澄清其与QED真空中非厄米现象的关系，并理解实电场与虚磁场耦合所引发的新物理效应。

Method: 在实电场与平行虚磁场并存背景下，计算标量和狄拉克QED的一圈有效拉氏量，采用原时（proper-time）表示并分析其被积函数的极点结构；研究两极点族在场强比为有理数时的重合与双重极点处理，并考察电场极点贡献对应的复Landau能级结构。

Result: 虚磁场在有效拉氏量原时表示的被积函数中产生额外极点族；即使无电场，虚磁场单独也能给出非零虚部；实电场与虚磁场贡献之间存在抵消机制；当两场强之比为有理数时部分极点重合，需仔细处理双重极点；每个Landau能级的真空衰变率随场强呈正弦式行为，其总和决定总体场依赖。

Conclusion: 本文厘清了虚磁场背景下QED有效拉氏量的解析结构，指出虚磁场的非厄米特性导致有效拉氏量即使无电场也出现虚部，并揭示了电场与虚磁场间的抵消效应及双重极点问题，为进一步研究QED真空中的非厄米现象奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate a non-Hermitian Schwinger effect by analyzing a one-loop effective Lagrangian of scalar and Dirac quantum electrodynamics (QED) in the presence of a real electric field and a parallel imaginary magnetic field. We show that an imaginary magnetic field produces an additional pole family in the integrand of the proper-time representation. Unlike a real magnetic field, an imaginary magnetic field can generate a nonzero imaginary part of the effective Lagrangian even in the absence of an electric field. The interplay between the two fields leads to nontrivial phenomena, such as a cancellation mechanism between the contributions of two fields. We also point out that when the ratio of two field strengths is rational, some poles among the two families coincide, and a careful treatment of double poles is required. By further examining the complex Landau-level structure of the electric-pole contribution, we find that the field dependence of the vacuum decay rate from each Landau level exhibits a sinusoidal-like behavior, and their sum determines the overall field dependence. Our results clarify the analytic structure of the QED effective Lagrangian in imaginary magnetic backgrounds and provide a basis for investigating its relation to non-Hermitian phenomena in the QED vacuum.

</details>


### [107] [The Disordered Universe 2025: Statistical Systems Out of Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36997)
*Weam Abou Hamdan,Martí Berenguer Mimó,Rafael Carrasco Carmona,Silvia Pla,Omkar Shetye,Tarek Anous,Paolo Benincasa,Guilherme Leite Pimentel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是一份讲解量子自旋玻璃的讲义，以球面 p-自旋模型为核心，系统介绍其热力学与动力学性质，并将其拓展到量子情形，最终探讨复制对称破缺与无序在宇宙学和全息（黑洞碎裂）问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻璃中的复制对称破缺、无序与慢动力学等概念，与宇宙学和全息学中关于黑洞碎裂等开放问题存在深层联系；作者希望提供一套自洽、完整的理论工具，为这些跨学科现象提供可计算的“理论实验室”。

Method: 以球面 p-自旋模型为主轴，先建立其热力学与动力学的完整分析框架；引入副本法（replica method），分别讨论副本对称解与一步副本对称破缺解，刻画所得到的玻璃相；随后将框架推广到量子模型，借助欧氏与实时关联函数研究慢动力学涌现与量子关联的作用；最后将复制对称破缺、无序等概念映射到宇宙学与全息体系中的黑洞碎裂问题。

Result: 成功刻画了球面 p-自旋模型的玻璃相（含副本对称破缺结构），给出了量子模型中的慢动力学行为及量子关联的关联函数描述，并论证了自旋玻璃概念可作为研究宇宙学与全息中黑洞碎裂等现象的有用理论工具。

Conclusion: 自旋玻璃理论为宇宙学与全息学中的开放问题（尤其是黑洞碎裂）提供了有效的分析框架，球面 p-自旋模型的量子推广及其副本方法为理解无序与复制对称破缺在引力及相关系统中的角色奠定了基础。

Abstract: These lecture notes provide an introduction to quantum spin glasses, with a particular emphasis on their application to open problems in cosmology and holography. Focusing on the spherical $p$-spin model, we develop a complete and self-contained analysis of its thermodynamic and dynamical properties. We introduce the replica method, discuss both replica-symmetric and one-step replica symmetry-broken solutions, and characterise the resulting glassy phase. The framework is then extended to the quantum model, where we study the emergence of slow dynamics and the role of quantum correlations through Euclidean and real-time correlation functions. Finally, we explore how concepts such as replica symmetry breaking and disorder naturally arise in cosmological and holographic systems related to black hole fragmentation, highlighting spin glass models as useful theoretical laboratories for these phenomena. These notes are based on a series of lectures held during the La Ricotta Summer School: The Disordered Universe 2025 from 21 July to 1 August 2025.

</details>


### [108] [Hironaka decompositions in supersymmetric theories: an exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36094)
*Abhijit Gadde,Rajat Raj,Shivansh Tomar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究树级超对称上同调在何种条件下允许 Hironaka 分解，即在有限多个衍生算子和代数无关的玻色原初算子为多项式系数下的唯一展开。对对角模型给出了完整构造与计数，对 ABJM、Klebanov-Witten、两类 class-S 理论的具体分支构造了显式基，同时指出并非所有上同调都允许这种分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为超对称上同调提供一种系统化的组织结构：通过 Hironaka 分解，将上同调整理成若干"塔"，每个塔共享一组不受约束的玻色原初算子作为"穿衣"（dressings），从而简化对高维和复杂模型上同调环的描述与计算。

Method: 本文采用 Hironaka 分解的代数框架，即要求上同调环可唯一展开为有限多个二次（secondary）算子的线性组合，其系数为代数无关的玻色原初算子的多项式。对具有 N 个标记、d≥1 种玻色子和 f 种费米子的对角模型，作者显式构造了这一分解并推导出二次算子数量公式 (N!)^{d-1}2^{fN}。随后将该方法应用于具体例子：证明 ABJM 理论的标量分支环在任意秩和正整数 Chern-Simons 能级上都允许 Hironaka 分解；为 SU(2)×SU(2) Klebanov-Witten 标量环以及两个 class-S 理论的 Higgs 分支构造显式基。

Result: 对角模型的上同调允许 Hironaka 分解，其二次算子个数为 (N!)^{d-1}2^{fN}。ABJM 标量分支环在任意秩与正整数 Chern-Simons 能级上都支持 Hironaka 分解；Klebanov-Witten 的 SU(2)×SU(2) 标量环与两类 class-S 理论的 Higgs 分支也构造出了显式基。但反例显示该性质并非普遍成立：在 W=x²y² 的 Wess-Zumino 模型中，每个正度标量都会湮灭非零类 xy，而 x^n（n≥1）均属于上同调，这两点与基于标量原初算子的 Hironaka 分解相矛盾。

Conclusion: Hironaka 分解为一大类超对称上同调（包括对角模型、ABJM、Klebanov-Witten 及某些 class-S 分支）提供了有效的组织方式，但并非普适性质——Wess-Zumino 反例表明当标量原初算子不足以支撑分解（存在正度标量湮灭非零类等情形）时该结构会失效。

Abstract: We ask when tree-level supersymmetric cohomology admits a Hironaka decomposition: a unique expansion in finitely many secondary operators with polynomial coefficients in algebraically independent bosonic primaries. Such a decomposition organizes the cohomology into towers with a common set of unrestricted dressings. For diagonal models with $N$ labels, $d\geq1$ bosonic species and $f$ fermionic species, we construct this decomposition and obtain $(N!)^{d-1}2^{fN}$ secondaries. We also prove that the scalar-branch ring of ABJM theory admits a Hironaka decomposition at every rank and positive integer Chern--Simons level, and construct explicit bases for the $SU(2)\times SU(2)$ Klebanov--Witten scalar ring. We also do the same for the Higgs branches of two class-S theories. However, not all supersymmetric cohomologies admit a Hironaka decomposition. In the Wess--Zumino model with $W=x^2y^2$, every positive-degree scalar annihilates the nonzero class $xy$, while $x^n$ is in cohomology for every $n\geq 1$. These two properties rule out a Hironaka decomposition over scalar primaries.

</details>


### [109] [The Disordered Universe 2025: An Intro and a Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37160)
*Paolo Benincasa,Guilherme Leite Pimentel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是“无序宇宙2025”暑期学校的导论性综述，阐述了一个跨学科研究方向——利用宇宙学、散射振幅、无序复杂系统及开放量子系统等领域的理念与技术，从极晚期到极早期全域解决宇宙学开放问题，并展望构建覆盖所有尺度的早期宇宙微观理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学中诸多开放问题横跨从极红外（极晚期）到早期宇宙的所有尺度，单一的宇宙学工具不足以应对；作者认为需要借鉴看似无关领域的理念与技术（散射振幅的解析性质、开放量子系统、无序系统/自旋玻璃、非平衡统计物理等），并以宇宙学作为统一框架整合这些视角，从而构建一个可覆盖所有尺度的早期宇宙微观理论。

Method: 本文并非单一技术性研究，而是采用综述/导论的方法：作为La Ricotta暑期学校“The Disordered Universe 2025”的总体愿景论文，系统梳理并整合四大领域（开放量子系统、散射振幅与关联函数的解析性质、de Sitter宇宙的基础与量子方面、非平衡统计物理与自旋玻璃）的核心概念与技术，阐明它们如何共同汇入同一条宇宙学叙事，并论证这一视角的合理性。

Result: 论文给出了“无序宇宙”研究方向的全局图景：宇宙学成为连接散射振幅、开放量子系统、无序复杂系统与de Sitter量子引力等多个领域的统一者，各领域的概念与技术可被整合进同一宇宙学叙事中；并勾勒了未来研究前景，强调建立一个覆盖所有尺度的早期宇宙微观理论的重要性。

Conclusion: 作者认为将来自四个不同领域的理念与技术纳入统一的宇宙学框架是一个值得探索的新方向，宇宙学既能从中受益又能反过来充当统一者；构建一个涵盖所有尺度、具有微观基础的早期宇宙理论是至关重要且富有前景的目标。

Abstract: The Disordered Universe is a novel research direction that exploits ideas and techniques coming from apparently unrelated research areas, ranging from cosmology to scattering amplitudes in particle physics and disordered complex systems, with the aim of addressing open questions in cosmology at all scales, from the very infra-red (or very late time) to the early universe. This paper acts as a general introduction and vision for a set of lectures delivered at the first La Ricotta Summer School "The Disordered Universe 2025", held from 21 July to 10 August 2025. These lectures involved open quantum systems, analytic properties of scattering amplitudes and correlation functions, foundations and quantum aspects of the de Sitter universe, as well as statistical physics out of equilibrium and spin glasses. While the lecture notes related to each course taught at the school will be published separately and are inevitably devoted to both conceptual and technical aspects of each of these topics, the school was not meant to be a collage of independent topics: the present paper has the purpose of outlining the global vision, how cosmology ends up being a unifier and benefits from both their viewpoint and techniques, how all the concepts and techniques originally coming from these four different areas are part of the same cosmological narrative, why this point of view deserves to be explored, and drawing perspectives for the future. We consider it crucial to build a microscopic theory for the early universe and maybe one that embraces all scales.

</details>


### [110] [Landau singularity of the K3 maximal cut in black-hole scattering at fifth post-Minkowskian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36128)
*Matthew D. Schwartz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四圈（后闵可夫斯基五阶）黑洞散射积分中在约94%光速处出现的发散，证明该速度是K3最大切割的朗道奇点，并仅通过最大切割与边界极限（无需完整四圈被积函数的IBP约化）解析导出记忆贡献边界积分的首项系数，同时将K3与Calabi–Yau周期延拓至束缚轨道能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后闵可夫斯基展开目前进展到五阶（四圈），此阶偏转角在黑洞以约94%光速掠过时出现发散。该发散来自节点化的K3曲面的周期，但其在积分中的位置与物理意义尚未完全厘清；同时记忆贡献（引力波对检验质量造成的永久位移）的归一化仍有待确定，而完整四圈被积函数的IBP约化极其困难，亟需绕过这一瓶颈的方法。

Method: 作者证明该节点位于K3最大切割的积分轮廓上，从而将该速度确立为切割的朗道奇点；要求发散在未引入新无穷大的条件下相消，以固定记忆贡献（最多差一个常数）；利用最大切割与边界极限技术，直接导出记忆贡献边界积分的首项系数的闭形式；并将K3与Calabi–Yau周期解析延拓至束缚轨道能量（如并合前的旋近阶段），全程不依赖完整四圈被积函数的integration-by-parts约化。

Result: 证明94%光速处发散对应K3最大切割积分轮廓上的朗道奇点；发散相消条件将记忆贡献固定到一个常数；给出了记忆贡献边界积分首项系数的闭形式；完成了K3与Calabi–Yau周期向束缚轨道能量的解析延拓。

Conclusion: 通过最大切割和边界极限即可同时确定发散奇点的几何来源与记忆贡献的首项系数，避免了对完整四圈被积函数进行IBP约化，为高阶后闵可夫斯基黑洞散射与引力波记忆效应的精确计算提供了新途径，并将相关周期推至束缚轨道区域。

Abstract: Modeling the gravitational waves from merging black holes requires precise calculations of the two-body problem. The post-Minkowskian expansion of black-hole scattering, in powers of Newton's constant, now stands at fifth order, four loops. At this order one part of the deflection diverges when the black holes pass at about 94 percent of the speed of light. The divergence is known to come from periods of a K3 surface, which has a node at this speed. We show that the node lies on the integration contour of the K3 maximal cut, which makes this speed a Landau singularity of the cut. Requiring the divergence to cancel, without introducing new infinities, has been shown to fix the memory contribution, named for the permanent displacement that the waves leave in test masses, up to one constant. We derive the leading coefficients of its boundary integrals in closed form. We obtain both results from maximal cuts and boundary limits, without an integration-by-parts reduction of the full four-loop integrand. We also continue the K3 and Calabi--Yau periods of these integrals to the energies of bound orbits, as in the inspiral before a merger.

</details>


### [111] [The Disordered Universe 2025: Analytic Properties of Amplitudes and Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36271)
*Mattia Capuano,Chandramouli Chowdhury,Chantelle Esper,Alessandro Palazio,Jonah Stalknecht,Yong Sheng Yap,Andrew J. McLeod,Paolo Benincasa,Guilherme Leite Pimentel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本讲义系统介绍微扰量子场论中圈级散射振幅与关联函数解析性质的现代研究技术，涵盖 Landau 分析、幺正性与因果性约束，以及迭代积分与符号运算（symbol calculus）驱动的 bootstrap 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圈级费曼积分是构成微扰振幅与关联函数的普适基本单元，其解析结构（尤其是运动学奇点的位置与性质）对理解散射振幅的物理行为至关重要。作者希望将 Landau 分析、物理原理约束与 bootstrap 技术串联起来，提供一套从解析结构直接确定费曼积分函数形式的完整方法论。

Method: 首先发展 Landau 分析思想，用于定位费曼积分中运动学奇点的位置与性质；随后利用幺正性（unitarity）与因果性（causality）等基本原理约束费曼积分的解析结构，并给出实际操作这些约束的方法；最后将上述观察与迭代积分（iterated integrals）及其符号运算（symbol calculus）联系起来，展示如何用 bootstrap 方法仅凭解析结构知识确定费曼积分的函数形式。

Result: 建立了一套从 Landau 奇点分析出发，经由幺正性与因果性约束，最终借助迭代积分与符号运算的 bootstrap 框架来确定圈级费曼积分函数形式的系统方法，为微扰量子场论中振幅与关联函数的解析结构研究提供了实用工具。

Conclusion: 现代解析技术将 Landau 奇点分析、物理基本原理（幺正性、因果性）与符号运算 bootstrap 方法相结合，使得仅凭费曼积分的解析结构知识即可系统地确定其函数形式；这些内容构成了 2025 年 La Ricotta 暑期学校“The Disordered Universe 2025”系列讲座的基础。

Abstract: These lectures provide an introduction to modern techniques for studying the analytic properties of loop-level scattering amplitudes and correlation functions in perturbative quantum field theory. We begin by developing the ideas behind Landau analysis, which identifies the location and nature of kinematic singularities in Feynman integrals (the universal building blocks that contribute to perturbative amplitudes and correlators). We then show how fundamental principles such as unitarity and causality constrain the analytic structure of Feynman integrals, and develop methods for working out some of these constraints in practice. Finally, we connect these observations to the properties of iterated integrals and their symbol calculus, to show how bootstrap methods can be used to determine the functional form of Feynman integrals from just knowledge of their analytic structure. These notes are based on a series of lectures held during the La Ricotta Summer School "The Disordered Universe 2025" from 21~July to 1~August~2025.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum hydrodynamics from Nambu's unpublished fluid notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36275)
*Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于南部（Nambu）未发表笔记《流体动力学新观》，构建量子流体动力学理论，将流体未观测模式溯源后得到作用于观测变形的量子信道，并研究量子相干性的保持与衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将流体动力学量子化并揭示观测变形与未观测流体模式之间的耦合如何影响量子相干性，特别是理解机械联络曲率（mechanical connection curvature）对相干保持的阻碍作用，以及不同初态制备对退相干的影响。

Method: 以动力学作用量定义位形空间上的度规与力学联络，把可观测量（cyclic deformation）耦合到未观测流体模式；通过对未观测模式求迹（tracing out）得到作用于观测变形的量子信道。建立动量反号间相干性精确保持的判据，分析联络曲率的作用；对两个剪切流识别精确无噪声符号子系统；引入额外生成流抵消欧拉方程初始残差并产生非零联络曲率。

Result: 得到相干性二次衰减的主导系数由制备的量子态决定；在固定平均能量下，选取与主导耦合流适配的振幅可显著降低初始退相干，即便在抵消欧拉残差的方向上；该优势在条件演化下逐渐减弱，最终导致退相干曲线交叉。实例表明初始相干衰减系数较小的制备在有限读出区间内可产生更大的最大采样损耗。

Conclusion: 通过量子信道框架量化了流体未观测模式导致的退相干，证明联络曲率非零时耦合不可消除；相干衰减由初态与几何（度规、曲率）共同决定，且初始衰减系数与有限时间最大损耗之间并无单调关系。

Abstract: We develop a quantum hydrodynamic theory based on Nambu's unpublished note \emph{A New Look at Fluid Dynamics}. The kinetic action defines a metric on configuration space and a mechanical connection coupling an observable cyclic deformation to unobserved fluid modes. Tracing out these modes yields a quantum channel for the observed deformation. We establish a criterion for the exact preservation of coherence between opposite momenta and show how nonzero connection curvature prevents this coupling from being removed. The leading quadratic decay of quantum coherence is determined by the prepared quantum state. For two shear flows, we identify an exact noiseless sign subsystem. Introducing additional generating flows cancels the initial residual of the Euler equations while producing nonzero connection curvature, governed by the same kinetic integrals that determine the classical correction. At fixed mean energy, choosing amplitudes adapted to the dominant coupling flow substantially reduces the initial decoherence, even along the direction that cancels the Euler residual. This advantage gradually diminishes under conditional evolution, eventually leading to a crossing of the decoherence curves. Our example shows that a preparation with a smaller initial coherence-decay coefficient can have a larger maximum sampled loss over a finite readout interval.

</details>


### [113] [The Disordered Universe 2025: Open Effective Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36898)
*Alessandro Fumagalli,Francesco Paronetto,Leonardo Stanzani,Mattia Varrone,Lucas Vicente García-Consuegra,Thomas Colas,Paolo Benincasa,Guilherme Leite Pimentel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文介绍开放有效场论（Open EFTs）框架，用 Schwinger-Keldysh 形式化方法刻画引力与宇宙学中不可观测环境带来的耗散与噪声效应，为在宏观有噪声、耗散环境下拓展传统有效场论提供系统可控的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有效场论源于粒子物理的干净、孤立初态实验，但真实宏观环境往往具有噪声与耗散特性。为克服传统有效场论在此类环境下的局限，并回应近来高能物理与凝聚态物理交叉领域的进展，需要构建一种将引力部分视为在不可观测介质中演化的新方法。

Method: 采用开放有效场论（Open Effective Field Theories）作为系统可控框架，运用 Schwinger-Keldysh 形式化方法捕捉未知环境的影响，将其表现为耗散与噪声，并应用于引力与宇宙学的观测探针。

Result: 建立了一套系统的开放有效场论框架，展示 Schwinger-Keldysh 形式化如何刻画未知环境对观测探针的耗散和噪声影响，为引力与宇宙学建模提供了可控的新途径。

Conclusion: 开放有效场论为在噪声与耗散环境下研究引力与宇宙学提供了系统且可控的框架，Schwinger-Keldysh 形式化方法是捕捉不可观测环境影响的通用工具；本章内容来源于 2025 年 La Ricotta 暑期学校“The Disordered Universe 2025”系列讲座。

Abstract: Effective field theories (EFTs) offer a powerful method to unify diverse models under a small set of control parameters, allowing systematic expansions around well-established theories. While these techniques were traditionally developed for particle physics experiments with clean, isolated initial states, realistic macroscopic environments are often noisy and dissipative. Recognising the limitations of traditional EFTs has recently sparked intense progress at the interface of high-energy physics and condensed matter. These developments motivate a new approach to gravitation and cosmology, one that models the gravitational sector as evolving in the presence of an unobservable medium. Open Effective Field Theories provide a systematic, controllable framework for this endeavour. This chapter presents this versatile framework and illustrates how the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism captures the influence of unknown environments, manifesting as dissipation and noise, on observational probes. These notes are based on a series of lectures held during the La Ricotta Summer School "The Disordered Universe 2025" from 21 July to 1 August 2025.

</details>


### [114] [Trace Relations and Partition Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36455)
*Robert de Mello Koch,Antal Jevicki,Junjie Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论矩阵模型中迹关系（trace relations）对有限 N 理论的作用：它们既导致大 N 希尔伯特空间的约化，也诱导出一类数量随 N² 指数增长的"奇迹态"（miraculous states），并探讨这些态在有限 N 热力学中及约束配分函数方面的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迹关系在有限 N 矩阵模型与集体场论中普遍出现，此前已发现它们会导致大 N 希尔伯特空间的约化；本文进一步关注这些迹关系诱导出的、数量随 N² 指数增长的奇迹态在有限 N 热力学中的作用，以及它们如何约束有限 N 理论的配分函数。

Method: 基于此前对迹关系导致大 N 希尔伯特空间约化的结论，作者考察由迹关系诱导出的新态（数量随 N² 指数增长），并分析它们与有限 N 热力学的关联及其对配分函数的约束作用。

Result: 本文讨论了奇迹态与有限 N 热力学的相关性，以及它们在约束有限 N 理论配分函数中所扮演的角色。

Conclusion: 迹关系诱导的奇迹态不仅导致大 N 希尔伯特空间的约化，还对有限 N 体系的热力学行为和配分函数构成重要约束，其数量随 N² 指数增长凸显了它们在有限 N 理论中的关键地位。

Abstract: In general trace relations appear in finite $N$ matrix models and collective field theories. They lead, as was established previously, to reductions of large $N$ Hilbert spaces. Even more importantly they induce the appearance of a whole class of new (miraculous) states whose number grows exponentially in $N^2$. In this work we discuss their relevance to finite $N$ thermodynamics and the role they play in constraining the partition functions of finite $N$ theories.

</details>


### [115] [The heterotic superpotential and the alpha' expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36604)
*Jock McOrist*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 Hull 联络在 N=1、d=4 真空的 heterotic 超势中的作用，证明由 H 参与的引力 Chern–Simons 耦合所定义的递归结构，是 W 在 α'^3 阶再现 Bergshoeff–de Roo 十维超对称条件的关键，并探讨将 Hull 与 Strominger 的某些张量方程推广到 α' 任意阶的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heterotic 弦理论的四维有效超势 W 的积分核用 H 定义，而 H 又通过引力 Chern–Simons 耦合依赖于 H，这种递归定义结构在超对称条件中的作用尚不清楚。作者希望阐明这一递归结构为何能使 W 在 α'^3 阶重现十维超对称条件，并由此拓展 Hull–Strominger 方程。

Method: 以 Hull 联络为核心，考察 heterotic 超势积分核的递归定义（H 通过含 H 的引力 Chern–Simons 耦合自洽定义）；将该递归结构与 Bergshoeff–de Roo 给出的十维超对称条件在 α'^3 阶进行对照，验证 W 能否重现这些条件；并在此基础上分析 Hull 与 Strominger 的张量方程向 α' 全阶推广的可能。

Result: 证明文中所述的递归结构（H 由其自身参与的引力 Chern–Simons 耦合定义）是超势 W 在 α'^3 阶再现 Bergshoeff–de Roo 十维超对称条件的关键；该结果支持将 Hull 与 Strominger 的某些张量方程推广到 α' 的任意阶。

Conclusion: Hull 联络通过 H 的递归 Chern–Simons 定义在 heterotic 超势中起核心作用，使得 W 能部分重现 Bergshoeff–de Roo 的十维超对称条件，这为进一步将 Hull–Strominger 张量方程推广到 α' 全阶提供了证据与方向。

Abstract: We study the role of the Hull connection in the heterotic superpotential for N=1 d=4 vacua. The integrand is defined in terms of H, which is in turn defined in terms of a gravitational Chern--Simons coupling involving H. We show this recursive structure is key to W reproducing some of the ten--dimensional supersymmetry conditions written down by Bergshoeff--de Roo through α'^3. With this evidence in hand we make some observations about extending some of the tensor equations of Hull and Strominger to all orders in α'.

</details>


### [116] [The Wess-Zumino Action of the Conformal Anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36735)
*Emil Mottola*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出二维与四维共形反常的完整 Wess-Zumino 作用量与应力-能量张量，用一个标量共形子场 φ 表述，解决共形规范固定模糊性，并揭示反常的红外起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前"积分反常"方法存在共形规范固定的模糊性，且未完整处理 WZ 一致性与红外物理；作者希望用单一标量 Nambu-Goldstone 共形子场 φ 给出完整的局部 WZ 有效作用量与应力-能量张量，并阐明反常的红外（IR）起源。

Method: 将 φ 参数化作用在时空度规上的局部阿贝尔 Weyl 群（g_{μν}→g_{μν}e^{-φ}），构造满足 WZ 一致性的局部有效作用量，并引入关于 φ 的变分原理以确定与无反常时空相关联的共形变换。在 D=2 中给出 Polyakov 作用量的局部形式，研究 SET 极化函数 ⟨TT⟩ 的虚部求和规则与实部光锥传播子；在 D=4 中分析 □R 项作为上同调平凡的上闭链及其红外极限贡献。

Result: D=2 的 WZ 作用量即 Polyakov 作用量的局部形式，其无质量 φ 传播子描述 SET 极化函数 ⟨TT⟩ 中的关联双粒子标量中间态；虚部满足紫外有限求和规则，对共形量子场在零能处成为 Dirac δ 函数，证明反常源于红外；实部成为 φ 的光锥传播子。D=4 中 □R 项虽对应上同调平凡上闭链，但也来自无质量量子场的红外极限，独立于紫外物理，并对低能引力的 WZ 作用量有贡献。

Conclusion: 类似于低能 QCD 中轴反常产生的膺 Goldstone 介子 WZ 作用量，共形反常的 WZ 作用量为宏观引力提供一个 Nambu-Goldstone 标量场，作为经典广义相对论度规之外的附加自由度。

Abstract: The complete Wess-Zumino (WZ) action and stress-energy tensor (SET) of the conformal anomaly in both two and four dimensions is given. This local effective action is expressed in terms of a single scalar Nambu-Goldstone `conformalon' field $\varphi$, parametrizing the local abelian Weyl group of conformal transformations that act transitively on the spacetime metric. Satisfying WZ consistency, and equipped with a variational principle for $\varphi$ that determines the conformal transformation $g_{μν}\!\to\!g_{μν}e^{-\varphi}$ to an associated spacetime with no anomaly, the WZ action resolves the conformal gauge fixing ambiguity of earlier methods of `integrating the anomaly.' In $D\!=\!2$ the WZ action is the local form of the Polyakov action, whose massless $\varphi$ propagator describes a correlated two-particle scalar intermediate state in the SET polarization function $\langle TT\rangle$. The imaginary part of this polarization obeys a UV finite sum rule, becoming a Dirac $δ$-function at zero energy for conformal quantum fields, demonstrating the infrared origin of the anomaly, while the real part becomes the light cone propagator of $\varphi$. Although the $\Box R$ term in $D\!=\!4$ corresponds to a cohomologically trivial cocycle, it also arises from the IR limit of massless quantum fields, independently of UV physics, and contributes to the WZ action for low energy gravity. Analogous to the WZ action for pseudo-Goldstone mesons in low energy QCD from the axial anomaly, the WZ action for the conformal anomaly provides a Nambu-Goldstone scalar field for macroscopic gravity, in addition to the metric of classical general relativity.

</details>


### [117] [MPS overlaps and Sklyanin determinants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37285)
*Tamas Gombor*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文推导出与正交和辛扭Yangian的任意有限维不可约赋值表示相关的可积矩阵乘积态（MPS）的显式重叠函数，其核心是把通用重叠公式中的对易拟行列式与嵌套子代数的Sklyanin行列式联系起来，并借助Gelfand–Tsetlin基与辛情形下的因子化技巧求得谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可积矩阵乘积态的重叠计算中，通用公式涉及对易拟行列式，但其谱在一般表示下并不显式。作者希望为正交与辛扭Yangian的有限维不可约赋值表示给出显式、可计算的重叠函数，以得到归一化的MPS重叠。

Method: 将通用重叠公式中出现的对易拟行列式与嵌套子代数的Sklyanin行列式建立联系。对正交赋值表示，该关系直接在Gelfand–Tsetlin基下确定其谱；对辛情形，将Sklyanin行列式与子式相结合，并对嵌套的二阶边界矩阵进行因子化，从而得到相应谱。所得重叠函数为按Gelfand–Tsetlin图样参数化的显式乘积，只需底层表示的最高权数据。

Result: 得到了正交与辛情形下显式的重叠函数，它们是由Gelfand–Tsetlin图样参数化的乘积，仅依赖正交或辛表示的最高权数据。结合Gaudin行列式的通用比值与模型相关的赝真空重叠系数，即可确定归一化的MPS重叠。同时，在正交情形下还将赋值表示以外的重叠函数用沿嵌套扭Yangian子代数出现的最高权表示出来。

Conclusion: 通过把对易拟行列式化归为Sklyanin行列式（正交情形直接取谱，辛情形辅以二阶边界矩阵因子化），成功导出可积MPS的显式重叠函数，仅需最高权数据即可计算，从而完成归一化重叠的确定。

Abstract: We derive explicit overlap functions for integrable matrix product states associated with arbitrary finite-dimensional irreducible evaluation representations of the orthogonal and symplectic twisted Yangians. The central step is to relate the commuting quasi-determinants entering the universal overlap formula to Sklyanin determinants of nested subalgebras. For orthogonal evaluation representations, this relation determines their spectra directly in the Gelfand--Tsetlin basis. In the symplectic case, we combine Sklyanin determinants and minors with a factorization of the nested rank-two boundary matrices to obtain the corresponding spectra. The resulting overlap functions are explicit products parametrized by Gelfand--Tsetlin patterns and require only the highest-weight data of the underlying orthogonal or symplectic representation. Together with the universal ratio of Gaudin determinants and the model-dependent pseudovacuum overlap coefficients, they determine the normalized MPS overlaps. We also express the overlap functions in the orthogonal case beyond evaluation representations in terms of the highest weights arising along the nested twisted-Yangian subalgebras.

</details>


### [118] [Wilson loops of Chern-Simons-matter Theories with one parameter and Picard-Fuchs equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36845)
*Takao Suyama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过 Picard-Fuchs 方程研究含味（flavored）Chern-Simons 理论与 ABJ 理论中的 Wilson 圈，详细分析方程奇点结构并确定奇点附近的局域解，同时确定 Wilson 圈对参数的依赖关系；在含味 Chern-Simons 理论中发现一种有趣的相变，并利用递归关系确定 n 重缠绕 Wilson 圈的 n 依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将此前工作推广，利用 Picard-Fuchs 方程研究含味 Chern-Simons 理论与 ABJ 理论中 Wilson 圈的行为，以理解其奇点结构、参数依赖及相变现象。

Method: 运用 Picard-Fuchs 方程，详细分析方程的奇点结构，确定奇点附近的局域解；利用此前工作得到的递归关系确定 n 重缠绕 Wilson 圈的 n 依赖。

Result: 确定了各理论单一参数的参数依赖；在含味 Chern-Simons 理论中发现一个有趣的相变；确定了 n 重缠绕 Wilson 圈的 n 依赖。

Conclusion: 通过 Picard-Fuchs 方程成功刻画了含味 Chern-Simons 理论与 ABJ 理论中 Wilson 圈的奇点结构、局域解及参数依赖，并揭示了含味 Chern-Simons 理论中的相变现象。

Abstract: Extending our previous work, we investigate Wilson loops of flavored Chern-Simons theory and ABJ theory by the use of Picard-Fuchs equations. The singularity structure of the equations is analyzed in detail, and local solutions around the singularities are determined. Each theory has one parameter, and the parameter dependence of the Wilson loops is also determined. In flavored Chern-Simons theory, we find an interesting phase transition. For $n$-times-wound Wilson loops, we determine their $n$-dependence by using a recursion relation obtained in our previous work.

</details>


### [119] [Reflected constraint algebras of open Carroll--Weyl null strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37546)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为开弦 Carroll–Weyl 零弦在目标原点 Dirichlet 条件或线性混合目标粘合下，构造了经典反射相空间，证明其反射相容约束可微、Hamilton 流保持边界域、约束系统在正则部分弱一阶类，并给出边界代数、嵌入计数、混合扇区、特征端点与缺陷行为的系列结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为开弦（带边界）情形下的 Carroll–Weyl 零弦建立与闭弦理论相容的反射相空间框架，从而获得可微的反射相容约束与由反射辛形式固定的边界代数。

Method: 构造经典反射相空间，施加目标原点 Dirichlet 条件或线性混合目标粘合；分析约束的可微性、Hamilton 流的边界保持性及弱一阶类性质；利用反射辛形式导出边界代数并归一化；通过局部正则嵌入计数、混合扇区构造、固定点反射、缺陷切向变分及位移 Dirichlet 数据的仿射提升进行研究。

Result: 约束系统在正则部分弱一阶类；边界代数将边界 Carrollian 共形代数扩展一个偶、权为 1 的 Carroll–Weyl 流，归一化由反射辛形式固定；正则局部嵌入计数与闭弦理论一致；构造出四维混合扇区（一个正则场对）与携带非零守恒目标空间 boost 荷、辛拉回非退化的解族；光滑固定点反射选取特征端点；类时缺陷在自然切向变分且无附加边界相互作用下，非特征端点 Carroll 流为零；对位移 Dirichlet 数据导出正则仿射提升及边界动量需按积分体动量平移。

Conclusion: 该反射相空间框架成功将闭弦 Carroll–Weyl 零弦理论推广到开弦/带边界情形，约束系统良定义且边界代数结构清晰，混合扇区、特征端点、缺陷行为与位移数据提升等结论为该开弦理论的进一步研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We construct a classical reflected phase space for open Carroll--Weyl null strings with Dirichlet conditions at the target origin or linear mixed target gluings. The reflection-compatible constraints are differentiable, their Hamiltonian flows preserve the boundary domain, and the constraint system is weakly first class on its regular part. The induced boundary algebra extends the boundary Carrollian conformal algebra by an even weight-one Carroll--Weyl current, with normalization fixed by the reflected symplectic form. The regular local embedding count agrees with that of the closed theory. We exhibit a four-dimensional mixed sector with one canonical field pair and a separate solution family carrying a nonzero conserved target-space boost charge and a nondegenerate symplectic pullback. Smooth fixed-point reflection selects characteristic endpoints. For timelike defects with natural tangential variations and no additional boundary interactions, non-characteristic endpoints have vanishing Carroll flow. For displaced Dirichlet data, we derive the canonical affine lift and its required shift of the edge momentum by integrated bulk momentum.

</details>


### [120] [Momentum-space entanglement and mutual information in Kaluza-Klein reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37009)
*Udaykrishna Thattarampilly,Yunlong Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究含紧致额外维理论（五维标量场在 M4×S1 上，带立方或四次自相互作用）经 Kaluza-Klein 约化后，四维观测者所见约化密度矩阵的动量空间纠缠。在耦合领头阶计算零模与单个 KK 能级间的互信息：立方理论中互信息以 1/mn 衰减且与紫外截断无关，与整个塔的互信息随能级数对数增长；四次理论中该尾巴被含额硬量子的态掩盖而与截断有关。结论是塔对低能振幅的修正被最轻能级主导并被局域算子捕捉，但互信息并非由有限能级集主导，源自有效作用量的时间非局域部分，故局域四维有效理论能描述低能可观测量，却无法描述约化系统的态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 Kaluza-Klein 约化中，约化密度矩阵（对质量塔求迹后）的非纯性及其动量空间纠缠，以揭示高维真空在四维有效描述中丢失的量子信息，并厘清局域有效场论能否刻画约化系统的态。

Method: 考虑 M4×S1 上带立方或四次自相互作用的五维标量场，对高维真空对塔模式求迹得约化密度矩阵；在耦合领头阶计算零模（分辨率标度 μ 以下）与单个 KK 能级 n 之间的互信息 In，并分析其随质量 mn、紫外截断、紧凑化半径 R 的标度关系，对比塔对低能振幅的修正与有效作用量的时间非局域部分。

Result: 立方理论中 In 以 1/mn 衰减，与紫外截断无关，系数为 (μR)^2/1024π^3（R 为紧凑化半径），与整个塔的互信息随能级数对数增长；四次理论中该尾巴被含额外硬量子的态掩盖，使 In 依赖截断。塔对低能振幅的修正由最轻能级主导并被局域算子捕捉，而互信息不由任何有限能级集主导，源自有效作用量的时间非局域部分。未破缺超对称抵消了紧致化的真空能，但不抵消互信息，领头阶互信息为振幅平方之和。

Conclusion: 局域四维有效理论能够描述约化系统的低能可观测量，但无法描述其态（即非局域的量子纠缠）。互信息与真空能的物理地位不同：超对称可抵消真空能却无法消除互信息。这揭示了有效场论在捕捉约化密度矩阵量子关联层面的局限。

Abstract: In theories with compact extra dimensions, the four-dimensional effective description is obtained by Kaluza-Klein reduction. The state available to a four-dimensional observer is the reduced density matrix obtained by tracing the higher-dimensional vacuum over the tower of massive modes, and it need not be pure. In this paper we study the momentum-space entanglement of this state for a five-dimensional scalar on $M_4\times S^1$ with a cubic or quartic self-interaction. We compute, at leading order in the coupling, the mutual information $I_n$ between the zero modes below a resolution scale $μ$ and a single Kaluza-Klein level $n$. When the zero modes couple linearly and without derivatives to pairs of tower modes, $I_n$ falls as $1/m_n$, where $m_n$ is the mass of level $n$. In the cubic theory, this tail is independent of the ultraviolet cutoff, with coefficient $(μR)^2/1024π^3$ for compactification radius $R$, and the mutual information with the whole tower grows logarithmically with the number of levels. In the quartic theory, the tail is masked by states containing additional hard quanta, which make $I_n$ depend on the cutoff. Compared with the corrections the tower makes to low-energy amplitudes, which are dominated by the lightest levels and captured by local operators, the mutual information with the tower is not dominated by any finite set of levels and arises from the time-nonlocal part of the effective action. The local four-dimensional effective theory therefore describes the low-energy observables of the reduced system but not its state. Unbroken supersymmetry cancels the vacuum energy of the compactification but not the mutual information, which at leading order is a sum of squared amplitudes.

</details>


### [121] [Another Brick in the Wall: Spectral Form Factor of Normal Modes in Horizonless Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37038)
*A. I. Belokon,M. Čubrović,V. Đukić,V. V. Pushkarev,L. Umrikhin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 作者计算了光滑无地平线背景下探针标量波的正则模谱及其谱形因子，发现砖墙模型即使出现线性斜坡也并非源于量子混沌，而真实微态几何（superstratum）则展现出斜坡与能级排斥等混沌迹象，说明正则模谱可用于研究 fuzzball 的量子动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黑洞微态（fuzzball）的量子混沌特征，判断砖墙模型能否刻画有限温度半经典黑洞微态中的能级关联，并考察正则模谱作为探测 fuzzball 量子动力学工具的可行性。

Method: 通过计算光滑无地平线背景下探针标量波的正则模（normal mode）谱及其谱形因子（spectral form factor）。先分析带砖墙遮蔽近地平线区域的 BTZ 黑洞背景，再对真实的微态几何（superstratum）求解正则模谱，并检验斜坡与能级排斥等量子混沌信号。

Result: 在砖墙模型中，谱形因子是否出现线性斜坡取决于砖墙构造的具体细节，但即便出现斜坡也不源自量子混沌的能级关联；而在 superstratum 微态几何中发现了斜坡与能级排斥，表明存在混沌迹象。

Conclusion: 正则模的本征值谱可以作为研究 fuzzball 量子动力学的有效工具，但砖墙模型无法复现这一混沌现象。

Abstract: We compute the normal mode spectrum and its spectral form factor for a probe scalar wave in smooth horizonless backgrounds. We first consider the BTZ black hole with a brickwall that hides the near-horizon region, which has recently been proposed as a toy model for a finite-temperature semiclassical black hole microstate. In this setup, the spectral form factor may or may not have a linear ramp, depending on the details of the brickwall construction. However, we argue that even in cases that do show a ramp, this ramp does not originate from level correlations associated with quantum chaos. Then we calculate the normal mode spectrum for a genuine microstate geometry (superstratum) and find evidence of chaos, both with a ramp and level repulsion. We conclude that the eigenvalue spectrum of normal modes can indeed serve as a tool for investigating the quantum dynamics of fuzzballs; however, the brickwall models are incapable of capturing this phenomenon.

</details>


### [122] [Scattering amplitudes from geometry of kernel-free functionals on piecewise random null paths with memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37064)
*Hiren Kakkad,Piotr Kotko*

Main category: hep-th

Abstract: We introduce a simple functional of algebra-valued fields with definite Lorentz properties that is kernel-free yet it encodes the structure of tree-level scattering amplitudes. We show that momentum-space reduction of the functional generates the cyclic Parke--Taylor factor, off shell in terms of light-front bilinears and on shell in terms of spinor brackets. The same reduction, organized differently, can be naturally promoted to twistor space without any twistor-space input: a closed cyclic chain of Penrose transforms on a twistor line, in which the Parke--Taylor denominators arise as boundary terms of the integrals along the paths. Finally we show that derivatives with respect to one of the path parameter generate numerators that together with the Parke-Taylor denominator build scattering amplitudes. Lorentz invariance fixes the spins of the inserted fields and the homogeneities of the corresponding twistor functions without any use of supersymmetry. The three-point outputs reproduce the kinematic forms of the light-cone cubic vertices of chiral higher-spin theory, and the derivative monomials of order four generate the components of the $\overline{\rm MHV}$ supermultiplet of $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory, off shell and on shell.

Abstract: We revisit the 2-loop computation of the partition function of the GS superstring expanded near the AdS$_2$ surface in AdS$_5\times S^5$ and AdS$_4\times {\rm CP}^3$, dual to $\frac{1}{2}$ BPS circular Wilson loop in ${\cal N}=4$ SYM and ABJM theories. In arXiv:2601.08809 it was done in AdS$_5\times S^5$ using dimensional regularization and a UV pole was found. Since dimensional regularization is problematic for the GS string, here we use a heat-kernel cutoff instead. Then the contribution of the local path integral measure, proportional to $δ(0)$, can no longer be ignored. We show that it cancels the $δ(0)$ terms coming from the quartic interactions in the action, leaving the square of the difference ${\rm Str}\,E$ between the regularized numbers of bosonic and fermionic modes. The result is thus UV finite, the pole of arXiv:2601.08809 being an artefact of dimensional regularization. If all fields are regularized with the same smearing kernel, ${\rm Str}\,E=0$ and the 2-loop coefficient $f_2$ vanishes, in tension with $f_2=\frac{3}{32π^2}$ in SYM theory. In the AdS$_4\times{\rm CP}^3$ case the same cancellation leaves a finite term proportional to the square of the coincident value of the massless 2d fermion Green's function, giving, ironically, $f_2=\frac{3}{32π^2}$ instead of $f_2=0$ required by localization. This term is the level-zero (string) part of the result of arXiv:2609.14497 for the AdS$_2\times S^1$ M2 brane in AdS$_4\times S^7/\mathbb Z_k$, where it is cancelled by the higher $S^1$ modes, which also remove a finite mass shift of the massless fermion in conflict with the AdS$_2$ supersymmetry. This suggests that the type II string path integral measure should contain an extra contribution, supplied in the IIA case by the parent M2 brane; in the AdS$_5\times S^5$ case it is still to be identified and may resolve the remaining 2-loop mismatch with localization.

</details>

Result: 讲义系统呈现了de Sitter时空中量子引力与宇宙学的已知结果与理论现状，并明确指出多个尚未解决的开放问题。这些开放问题为理解Λ>0宇宙中的量子引力和宇宙学指明了持续努力的方向，但完整的数学框架和底层自由度描述仍未建立。

Abstract: A complete understanding of cosmology, from its earliest epoch all the way to the current dark energy dominated era, requires the development of a mathematical framework that incorporates quantum features in an expanding universe. Such a framework, alongside a description of the underlying degrees of freedom, is so far lacking. Several of the pertinent challenges and puzzles already arise upon considering even the most idealised model of an expanding universe --- the de Sitter spacetime and fluctuations thereof. In these lectures, we review some elementary ideas of the de Sitter physics, passing through the representation theory of the de Sitter group, aspects of canonical quantum gravity, all the way to attempts in describing quasi-local features. Throughout, we highlight open problems that motivate ongoing efforts to understand quantum gravity and cosmology in universes with $Λ>0$. These notes are based on a series of lectures held during the La Ricotta Summer School: The Disordered Universe 2025 from 21 July to 1 August 2025.

</details>

Conclusion: 该模型能产生两类带标量毛的稳态轴对称黑洞，II 型（scalaron 支撑型）黑洞具有强形变的标量结构和更大的视界半径，其支结构随引力耦合强度发生拓扑重连，显示标量毛与孤立子解之间存在深刻的对应关系。

Abstract: We construct stationary axially symmetric black holes with scalar hair in the gravitating nonlinear $O(3)$ sigma model with a partly negative symmetry-breaking potential. Our results show that it supports two qualitatively different types of asymptotically flat hairy black holes, distinguished by their connection to the corresponding solitonic branches and by the structure of their scalar hair; the type I black holes are connected to the vacuum branch and type II black holes are connected to regular scalaron-supported spinning solitons. The type II black holes have a strongly deformed scalar field structure, with the scalar field taking its vacuum value on the symmetry axis and approaching the anti-vacuum in the near-horizon equatorial region. We analyze their global charges, horizon properties, and dependence on the model parameters, comparing the solutions with Kerr black holes. For sufficiently weak gravitational coupling, the branch connectivity changes: the fundamental branches of types I and II reconnect to form one continuous family, while their corresponding second branches form another. We find that, in contrast to the vacuum-connected type I family, the scalaron-supported type II black holes extend to substantially larger horizon radii while preserving a nontrivial scalar configuration outside the horizon.

Result: 作者证明了两个电荷表示定理，并在两种情形（诺特通量消失、诺特通量可积）下给出中心扩张的显式表达式及其上闭链恒等式。作为应用，对满足 Penrose–Brown–Henneaux 边界条件的渐近局部 AdS₃ 时空，成功恢复了对应的中心扩张渐近对称代数，为电荷表示定理提供了显式实现。

Abstract: Central extensions of asymptotic symmetry algebras are traditionally determined through an explicit computation of the algebra of conserved charges. We show that, within an ambiguity-free covariant phase space formalism based on the Noetherian split, they instead arise as intrinsic quantities completely determined by the Lagrangian through the geometry of the associated covariant phase space. We prove two general Charge Representation Theorems describing the canonical realization of diffeomorphism symmetries in the presence of field-dependent vector fields and Lagrangian anomalies, corresponding respectively to vanishing and integrable Noetherian flux. In both cases, we derive explicit expressions for the associated central extensions and establish the corresponding cocycle identities. This provides a constructive algorithm for determining central charges directly from the Lagrangian formulation of the theory, without requiring an explicit computation of the charge algebra. As a non-trivial application, we consider asymptotically locally AdS$_3$ spacetimes with Penrose--Brown--Henneaux boundary conditions. We recover the corresponding centrally extended asymptotic symmetry algebra, providing an explicit realization of the Charge Representation Theorems.

Abstract: We propose bounds relating thermal entropy to entanglement in unitary conformal field theories: $f\le4π^2a$ in four dimensions and $f\le2.99830949\ldots\,F$ in three dimensions. Here $f$ is the dimensionless coefficient of the cosmological constant term in the thermal effective action, $a$ is the Euler anomaly coefficient, and $F$ is the sphere free energy. Spherical Rényi entropy relates the four-dimensional bound to the conformal collider bounds. We formulate a stronger conjectural inequality involving the stress-tensor three-point function, saturated by every free theory, and establish it in controlled regimes of weakly coupled gauge theories, large-$N$ vector models, and holographic CFTs. Finite-$N$ numerical results provide additional tests. The bounds constrain the Cardy growth of the density of states at high energy.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个系统框架，用于在标准Born-Infeld设定之外构造并分类精确的$T\bar T$-like流，统一了二维可积sigma模型与四维自对偶非线性电动力学的描述，并推广到任意维，为后续研究奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: Recently, a general exact autonomy framework for $T\bar{T}$-like deformations of 2D integrable sigma models and 4D nonlinear electrodynamics has been developed. The theories are characterized by the Courant-Hilbert equation and admit an equivalent description in the Russo-Townsend auxiliary-field formulation. For general one-scale families, we derive an exact irrelevant flow equation of the universal form $\partial_λ\mathcal{L}=X\mathcal{F}(\sqrt{Y/X}), \,\,\,X=T_{μν}T^{μν}, \,\,\, Y=(T^μ_μ)^2 $ where the function $\mathcal{F}$ is determined by the underlying Courant-Hilbert solution. We obtain a parametric representation valid for arbitrary one-scale models, establish its perturbative expansion in terms of the defining function or auxiliary potential, and identify the domain in which the flow remains regular. We also show that the irrelevant flow commutes with the marginal root-$T\bar{T}$ deformation and that both flows preserve classical integrability. Explicit results are derived for generalized Born-Infeld, q-deformed, no-$τ$-maximum, and logarithmic models, with the latter admitting a closed representation. Finally, we extend the flow construction to arbitrary spacetime dimension $(d)$, revealing a common one-variable structure that connects two-dimensional integrable sigma models to four-dimensional self-dual nonlinear electrodynamics. These results provide a systematic framework for constructing and classifying exact $T\bar{T}$-like flows beyond the standard Born-Infeld setting.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力单圈真空有效作用量对牛顿势产生量级为 $1/r^3$ 的领头量子修正，而单圈修正同时与迹异常相关。作者希望厘清迹异常积分所得有效作用量能否复现该修正，并进一步寻找能正确产生 $1/r^3$ 修正的完整半经典描述。

Method: 作者先基于其先前工作证明仅由迹异常积分得到的有效作用量无法复现 $1/r^3$ 修正。随后在 Schwarzschild 时空中采用 Visser 提出的真空极化应力张量近似，取 Boulware 真空并在大距离处展开，验证该应力张量守恒且复现迹异常。将异常应力张量与真空极化应力张量共同代入半经典爱因斯坦方程，按 $\hbar$ 微扰展开逐阶求解。

Result: Visser 近似能够产生对牛顿势的 $1/r^3$ 修正，且该修正对源质量 $M$ 线性，结构上与单圈结果一致。修正的系数由 Visser 多项式 ansatz 的单个自由参数控制，可通过调节该参数匹配单圈值。迹异常系数的依赖只出现在 $1/r^4$ 阶。

Conclusion: 完整的半经典爱因斯坦方程（含真空极化应力张量与异常应力张量）借助 Visser 近似可在参数可调条件下复现引力单圈给出的 $1/r^3$ 牛顿势修正；迹异常参数仅影响高阶（$1/r^4$）项，说明领头量子修正并非由迹异常单独决定。

Abstract: The leading quantum correction to the Newton potential induced by the one-loop vacuum effective action of gravity is of order $1/r^3$. One-loop corrections are also responsible for the trace anomaly. We first show, following our previous work, that the effective action obtained by integrating the trace anomaly cannot reproduce this correction. We then complete the semiclassical Einstein equations with the vacuum-polarization stress tensor in addition to the anomalous stress tensor. The full stress tensor follows from the approximation proposed by Visser for the Schwarzschild spacetime. The stress tensor is taken in the Boulware vacuum and expanded at large distance, where it is conserved and reproduces the trace anomaly. Solving the equations perturbatively in $\hbar$, we find that Visser's approximation generates the $1/r^3$ correction to the Newton potential, linear in the source mass $M$, with the same structure as the one-loop result. Its coefficient is controlled by a single free parameter of Visser's polynomial ansatz, which can be tuned to match the one-loop value. The dependence on the trace-anomaly coefficient appears only at order $1/r^4$.

</details>


### [130] [Resurgence of Black Holes in Thermal OPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37554)
*Alba Grassi,Hewei Frederic Jia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用精确WKB分析研究了全息共形场论中热二点函数高能渐近展开的复现(resurgence)结构，识别了Borel奇点位置、复频率腔室以及Stokes跳变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动空间热算子积展开(thermal OPE)对热二点函数给出渐近的高能展开，但其微扰展开不收敛，需要理解非微扰效应如何编码边界之外的整体几何信息，并考察该渐近级数的Borel可和性。

Method: 在无限与有限体积全息共形场论中（对偶为平面与球形AdS黑洞几何），将微扰展开由热OPE的应力张量扇区确定到所有阶，并通过体波动方程的近边界分析计算；采用精确WKB分析定位Borel奇点并推导Stokes跳变。

Result: 识别出Borel奇点位于AdS边界与复径向平面特殊点之间的WKB作用量处，在特定区域包括黑洞奇点；在类时动量区域的复频率腔室内，Borel重求和可重建完整热二点函数，并推导了穿越该腔室边界所需的非微扰贡献（Stokes跳变）。

Conclusion: 高能渐近展开经Borel重求和后可在特定复频率区域完整还原热二点函数，非微扰效应由远离边界的体几何信息决定，体现了全息对偶中微扰与非微扰结构的深刻联系。

Abstract: The momentum-space thermal OPE generically gives an asymptotic high-energy expansion of thermal two-point functions. We study the resurgent structure of this expansion in holographic CFTs at infinite and finite volume, whose dual bulk geometries are planar and spherical AdS black holes, respectively. The perturbative expansion is fixed to all orders by the stress-tensor sector of the thermal OPE and can be computed from a near-boundary analysis of the bulk wave equation. Non-perturbative effects, on the other hand, encode information about the bulk geometry away from the boundary. Using exact WKB analysis, we identify the locations of the associated Borel singularities as WKB actions between the AdS boundary and distinguished points in the complex radial plane, which include the black hole singularity in certain regimes. Furthermore, we identify a complex-frequency chamber in the timelike momentum regime where Borel resummation of the high-energy expansion reconstructs the full thermal two-point function. We further derive Stokes jumps that determine the non-perturbative contributions required upon crossing the boundary of this chamber.

</details>


### [131] [A Spin on Radiation Reaction with Cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37603)
*Asaad Elkhidir,Matteo Sergola,Pablo Vives Matasan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了自旋带电体（√Kerr 粒子）在自旋二次阶下的辐射反作用运动方程，推广了 Abraham–Lorentz–Dirac 方程，并用维度正规化去除自场的发散；随后分别用经典方法和振幅幺正切割方法计算散射过程中的单圈波形，二者在自旋二次阶内一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究自旋粒子在经典电动力学中的辐射反作用效应，并将此前借助康普顿振幅幺正切割编码辐射反作用的量子场论方法推广到自旋情形，通过对比经典与振幅两种方法的结果来检验运动方程的正确性。

Method: 作者推广 Coleman 的论证，通过计算自旋体在其自身世界线上的自场并利用维度正规化消除发散，得到自旋二次阶的辐射反作用方程；随后计算自旋体与旁观电荷散射所辐射的电磁波形（树级及含单圈辐射反作用修正），并利用基于康普顿振幅的幺正切割构造重新计算该单圈波形。

Result: 经典方法与振幅方法得到的波形在自旋二次阶内相符，从而验证了所推导的辐射反作用方程；同时该方法还复现了自旋线性阶自力和六维无自旋自力的已知结果。

Conclusion: 文章成功将辐射反作用的经典推导与振幅方法统一拓展至自旋二次阶，并通过波形一致性检验证实了辐射反作用方程的正确性，同时印证了对自旋线性阶及六维无自旋情形的既有结论。

Abstract: We derive the radiation-reaction equations of motion for a spinning charged body, the $\sqrt{\text{Kerr}}$ particle, through quadratic order in its spin, generalising the Abraham--Lorentz--Dirac equation. Extending an argument of Coleman, we obtain radiation reaction equations by evaluating the self-field of the spinning body on its own worldline, and removing its divergences with dimensional regularisation. We use the resulting equations of motion to compute the electromagnetic waveform emitted in the scattering of the spinning body off a spectator charge, both at tree level and including the leading radiation-reaction correction, which enters at one loop. Then, we apply the construction introduced in a previous work by some of the authors -- where radiation reaction is encoded in unitarity cuts involving Compton amplitudes -- to spinning particles, and recompute the radiation-reaction contribution to the one-loop waveform. We find that the waveform calculated using classical and amplitude methods agree through quadratic order in spin, providing a check on our radiation-reaction equations. Along the way, we also reproduce known results for linear-in-spin self-force effects and for the spinless self-force in six dimensions.

</details>


### [132] [Stability of Axion--SU(2) Chromomagnetic Condensates with Stochastic Spin-2 Dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37610)
*Sha Azyzy,Azadeh Maleknejad,Vahid Kamali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究随机自旋-2规范场背景能否在平坦时空中、固定轴子Noether荷的条件下稳定轴子–SU(2)杨-米尔斯理论的色彩磁凝聚。通过对规范场粗粒化，作者发现一类新的统计均匀且各向同性的自旋-2着装色磁凝聚，并先后在平均场（Hartree）层面和加入领头散射修正的层面分析其涨落，证明能隙存活受着装的非高斯关联控制，且模型无关的统计正定性约束可区分出能隙可能存活的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨自旋-2规范场背景能否稳定轴子–SU(2)杨-米尔斯理论的色彩磁凝聚，以解决此类非阿贝尔规范凝聚在宇宙学（暴胀、暗能量、原初引力波）及QCD类规范区中可能存在的稳定性问题。

Method: 对规范场进行粗粒化，构造统计均匀且各向同性的自旋-2着装色磁凝聚；先用平均场（Hartree）方法求涨落谱并确定所有模正能隙的区域；再计入涨落在随机背景中传播的领头散射修正，并用模型无关的统计正定性（非高斯关联的约束）条件分析能隙存活。

Result: 发现能隙存活由着装的非高斯关联控制；统计正定性通过模型无关的一致性条件约束未确定的高阶点关联，将Hartree正能隙区域划分为两部分：一部分在Born级能隙为正被排除，另一部分稳定性仍可能成立。

Conclusion: 随机自旋-2规范背景的非高斯关联对色磁凝聚的稳定性起关键作用，统计正定性提供了普适约束；结果适用于早期宇宙的非阿贝尔规范凝聚（暴胀、暗能量、原初引力波）及热效应次要的QCD类规范区。

Abstract: We investigate whether a stochastic spin-2 gauge-field background can stabilize the chromomagnetic condensate of axion--SU(2) Yang--Mills theory at fixed axion Noether charge in flat spacetime. By coarse-graining the gauge field, we uncover a new class of statistically homogeneous and isotropic spin-2-dressed chromomagnetic condensates satisfying the classical background equations. We then study their fluctuations in two steps. First, at the mean-field (Hartree) level, we determine the fluctuation spectrum and the region where all modes remain positively gapped. Second, we include the leading scattering corrections from fluctuations propagating through the stochastic background. We find that gap survival is controlled by non-Gaussian correlations of the dressing. Statistical positivity constrains the otherwise undetermined higher-point contributions through model-independent consistency conditions, without fixing their detailed momentum dependence. These bounds separate the Hartree-gapped region into a part where a positive Born-level gap is ruled out and a part where stability remains possible. The results are relevant to non-Abelian gauge condensates in early-universe cosmology, including inflation, dark energy, and primordial gravitational waves, and to QCD-like gauge sectors above confinement where thermal effects are subleading.

</details>


### [133] [The 't Hooft model on (A)dS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37667)
*Panos Betzios*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文求解了大 N 二维 QCD（'t Hooft 模型）在刚性 AdS₂ 和 dS₂ 上的介子谱，发现曲率通过 SL(2,ℝ) Casimir 修正 't Hooft 方程，得到精确的介子方程，并分析了对偶性、手征对称性与退禁闭/共振行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维 QCD 在弯曲时空（AdS₂/dS₂）中的行为，以理解曲率如何影响禁闭、手征对称性破缺、Regge 轨迹和介子稳定性，并建立与弦论、光前全息的联系。

Method: 利​​用 AdS₂/dS₂ 等距群的对称性保持零规范 A_-=0；在仿射坐标中，曲率仅通过零线特殊共形生成元进入光前哈密顿量；由 SL(2,ℝ) Casimir 导出精确介子方程，即 't Hooft 方程加一个 Legendre 算子（符号由曲率决定），并分别在两种时空求解谱与边界问题。

Result: 在 AdS₂ 上：π 介子在无质量极限下无质量、满足 Gell-Mann–Oakes–Renner 关系并固定手征凝聚；当弦长超过曲率半径时维度从 Regge 增长过渡为对数增长；平直时空的可积结构得以保留；并求解了夸克与边界反夸克的相互作用，超过临界耦合时弦将其拉回边界，产生散射相位。在 dS₂ 上：由于空间膨胀，介子即使 N=∞ 也成为不稳定共振并最终解离，其宽度由 de Sitter 温度的玻尔兹曼因子给出。

Conclusion: 曲率对二维 QCD 的禁闭动力学产生本质影响：AdS₂ 保持禁闭但改变渐近谱形态，dS₂ 则因膨胀导致介子退禁闭成共振，揭示了弯曲时空中色禁闭与热化行为的新机制。

Abstract: We solve large-$N$ two-dimensional QCD with fundamental quarks---the 't Hooft model---on rigid AdS$_2$ and dS$_2$. Their isometries preserve the null gauge $A_-=0$ and in affine coordinates the curvature enters the light-front Hamiltonian only through the special-conformal generator of the null line. The $SL(2,\mathbb{R)}$ Casimir then results in an exact meson equation: 't Hooft's equation plus a Legendre operator whose sign is determined by the curvature. On AdS$_2$ it is related to equations proposed in the context of folded/segmented strings and light-front holography. The pion is massless in the chiral limit and obeys a Gell-Mann--Oakes--Renner relation that fixes the chiral condensate, the dimensions cross over from Regge to logarithmic growth once the flux tube exceeds the curvature radius, and the flat-space integrable structure survives. We also solve the problem of a quark interacting with an antiquark placed at the boundary: beyond a critical coupling the string pulls it back to the boundary, resulting in a scattering phase. On dS$_2$ and due to the expansion of space, the mesons are unstable resonances even at $N=\infty$ and eventually dissociate. Their widths are given by Boltzmann factors at the de Sitter temperature.

</details>
